# RH global-coupling corpus audit

- **ID:** rh-global-coupling-corpus-audit
- **Stage:** 1-in-progress
- **Type:** ORIGINATION / CORPUS-WIDE AUDIT
- **Started:** 2026-08-23
- **Status:** ACTIVE

## Research question

Assume individual Xi zeros have no independent reason: the divisor is globally coupled and local
certificates are shadows of cross-zero compatibility. Find an unconditional, source-defined cover
of the off-line strip whose chartwise producer obligations are individually weaker, genuinely
complementary, and not a renamed RH endpoint.

No claim is frozen yet. This log precedes conjecture selection. It records the complete internal
novelty boundary needed to avoid rediscovery and endpoint theatre.

## Acceptance test

A surviving architecture must satisfy all of the following.

1. **Source-first:** charts and producer data are defined without selecting, enumerating, or tuning
   against Xi zeros.
2. **Cross-zero:** essential law couples at least two spectral locations, two charts, or a finite
   packet under a non-factorizing compatibility condition.
3. **Strictly local burden:** no fixed chart obligation implies RH, and this weakness is proved in
   a parent class or by an exact information-factorization theorem.
4. **Complementarity:** joint force comes from an explicit overlap/refinement/cocycle/conservation
   law, not from the tautology that every point lies in some box.
5. **No endpoint redressing:** replacing chart vocabulary by zero-set, Hankel, Weil, Li, Jensen,
   or positivity vocabulary must not reveal a pre-existing RH equivalence with identical content.
6. **Producer surface:** at least one source-side statement is stronger than the divisor conclusion
   and has a plausible attack independent of the target zero configuration.
7. **Closed Lean target:** first formal theorem is unconditional and closed; it introduces no RH
   premise, producer hypothesis, or new axiom.

## Audit schema

Every audited mechanism receives one row with these fields.

| Field | Meaning |
|---|---|
| Carrier | Source object before any divisor extraction |
| Localization | Point / pair / packet / window / scale / global |
| Coupling arity | Number or shape of coordinates linked irreducibly |
| Compatibility | Overlap, refinement, cocycle, conservation, determinant, projective, or limit law |
| Divisor feed | Exact first declaration where zeros enter |
| RH saturation | Whether one chart, finite family, countable family, or aggregate already carries RH |
| No-go | Formal theorem preventing naive gluing or local-to-global inference |
| Positive survivor | Strongest source-defined content left after obstruction |
| Open producer | Exact missing source theorem, if any |
| Verdict | SURVIVES / ENDPOINT / DOWNSTREAM / NONSELECTIVE / DUPLICATE / DEAD |

## Deterministic corpus pass

Lean corpus is inspected only through `scripts/formal_tools.py research`. Passes are declaration-
driven, not keyword improvisation.

1. Xi/zeta foundations and divisor representations.
2. Explicit-formula, prime--Gamma, contour, rectangle, and source-moment modules.
3. Weil/Hankel/Li/Jensen/Laguerre--Polya and finite-test reductions.
4. CCM, Deninger, Suzuki, Hedenmalm, Sonin, de Branges, Krein, resolvent, and operator carriers.
5. Ward/Green/Fisher/thermal/response and conservation-law carriers.
6. Sheaf, cover, cocycle, descent, Kummer, Cartier, formation, and local-to-global infrastructure.
7. Approximation, density, compactness, cofinality, and uniform-exchange guardrails.
8. Countermodels, surgery, mobility, nonselectivity, finite-source, and bounded-depth no-gos.
9. Adjacent HTGeometry mechanisms with irreducible multi-point or overlap compatibility.
10. Gap-marker census and umbrella/index cross-check.

Each pass records query, returned declarations, inspected modules, classification rows, and unresolved
follow-ups below. No arXiv search begins until passes 1--10 establish internal novelty boundary.

## Rejected theatre ledger

### R1. Pointwise universal certificates

`XiZeroLocalCriticalPhaseUniversalityLaw` proves an on-line zero simultaneously realizes eleven
charts. Therefore “each zero has a different local reason” is false in the naive pointwise sense.
Local phase certificates are receiver dictionaries, not producers.

**Verdict:** DOWNSTREAM / NONSELECTIVE.

### R2. Countable integer-Hankel witness atlas

`XiTraceHankelCountableCriterion` and `XiCountableHankelTestSetUniversalityLaw` already prove that
integer-polynomial and rational-rectangle finite tests saturate the full cone and are equivalent to
RH. Per-test Gram limits add no sign content.

**Verdict:** ENDPOINT.

### R3. Jensen first-failure atlas

Pólya's Jensen criterion makes failure of real-rootedness appear in some finite coefficient window,
while eventual hyperbolicity clears fixed degrees at high shift. This is a useful index-plane
frontier but remains the classical all-Jensen RH equivalence; a “first failed chart” is logical
negation, not a producer.

**Verdict:** DUPLICATE / ENDPOINT.

### R4. Rational isolating rectangles with nonzero source contour count

`CountableWindowBasis` already formalizes rational isolation of every off-seam divisor atom.
`XiArithmeticSourceMomentTorelli` identifies zeroth prime--Gamma contour moment with enclosed
multiplicity. Joining them merely localizes the argument principle and rephrases the already-known
countable window endpoint. Geometric coverage supplies no cross-chart compatibility.

**Verdict:** DOWNSTREAM / THEATRE.

### R5. Positive finite or countable channel splitting

`CCMRectangularFiniteChannelNonDilution` proves a finite positive decomposition leaves one channel
with full abscissa. `CCMSourceDivisorEnergySpectrum` proves a countable positive decomposition moves
the same burden to the aggregate. Splitting a positive RH-bearing quantity does not make obligations
weaker in a useful sense.

**Verdict:** DEAD; survivor must use signed/complex overlap data or a genuinely non-additive weld.

## Working hypothesis, not yet a conjecture

### Governing thesis

Existing RH programmes do not contain the sought producer. Suzuki/de Branges positivity, Deninger
cohomology and dynamics, Connes semilocal trace geometry, Weil/Hankel forms, Jensen/Laguerre--Polya
coefficient tests, Ward currents, and zero statistics are shadows of a larger and qualitatively
different law, object, or constraint. Their repeated arrival at exact RH-equivalent endpoints is
evidence of projection loss: each route can recognize a valid divisor after receiving it, but none
retains the pre-divisor compatibility that generates admissible divisors.

The audit therefore does **not** seek the best existing route or a hybrid conjunction of route
endpoints. It seeks an unknown parent category and the information destroyed by each shadow map.

Write provisionally

```text
                         unknown parent source object P
                       /       |        |        \
              Suzuki shadow  Deninger  Connes   Weil/Ward/... shadows
                       \       |        |        /
                         the same Xi divisor data
```

Each lower route may be faithful after universal quantification and still be useless for production.
The target datum lies in the kernel of every individual projection but not in the kernel of their
pre-scalarized joint compatibility. Candidate forms include a higher cocycle, descent obstruction,
transition 2-cell, determinant-line orientation, refinement anomaly, noncommutative incidence law,
or a new structure not represented by present vocabulary.

Zeros are not coordinates of `P` and do not receive separate proofs. They are singular events or
observable shadows forced by one global configuration of `P`. “Different reasons” may appear only
because different local probes expose different faces of the same parent constraint.

### Conceptual correction: zeros are failures, not outputs

The parent object must not be a mechanism that *creates* critical-line zeros. A zero is an error
locus: failure of invertibility, exactness, descent, comparison, factorization, or global
completion. The unknown law classifies which failures are admissible. RH should therefore read:

> every failure of the completed arithmetic object is neutral/balanced.

Different zeros may be genuinely different obstruction classes and may fail for different local
reasons. What is universal is not their producer but the conservation/compatibility law restricting
every admissible failure to the neutral grade. Consequently:

- a spectrum, determinant, or canonical product is downstream unless it independently constructs
  the object whose exactness or invertibility can fail;
- explaining why zeros exist or how they are distributed is not yet the target;
- a viable parent theory defines an error type before inserting the Xi divisor;
- critical-line symmetry must restrict error classes, not label already known zeros;
- an off-line contradiction must say that a non-neutral failure violates a global source law, not
  merely that a scalar endpoint becomes negative.

This is a hard audit criterion for every route below.

### Candidate interpretation under audit: completed topological defects

“Topological defect” is a mathematically testable interpretation; “soliton” is not yet earned.
Current corpus evidence suggests the following provisional model.

- Finite arithmetic stages are zero-free and carry trivial local monodromy; defect charge can be
  born only under infinite completion.
- A zero is a nonzero annihilator/error class of the completed source presentation. Multiplicity is
  its jet length or local integral charge.
- Ordinary local winding is insufficient: it records multiplicity but not horizontal position.
  Each defect therefore needs both an integral charge and a duality grade
  `Re(ρ) - 1/2` (or an intrinsic replacement for that scalar shadow).
- RH would state that every completed defect is self-dual/neutral-grade. Distinct zeros may carry
  different obstruction cocycles while satisfying the same global neutrality law.

To promote “defect” to “soliton”, the audit must independently find a source field/configuration
space, nonlinear evolution, localization, finite energy/action, topological sector, and stability.
Absent those data, soliton language is rejected as metaphor. A viable defect theory must also beat
the local-winding no-go by recovering the duality grade from global transition data.

### Consequences for corpus audit

For every route, record four additional items.

1. **Shadow map:** exact declaration where source structure is scalarized, decategorified, traced,
   determinant-reduced, or converted to divisor data.
2. **Lost kernel:** deformations invisible after that map, especially formalized countermodels.
3. **Shared invariant:** datum also appearing in at least one unrelated route before scalarization.
4. **Lift obstruction:** what would be required to reconstruct a common parent object from route
   shadows, and whether corpus proves such reconstruction impossible or incomplete.

Hybridizing terminal inequalities is forbidden. Only pre-shadow compatibility may survive.

This hypothesis survives only if corpus pass finds unconditional source constructions before the
divisor and a joint compatibility law not already equivalent to RH by scalarization.

## Pass log

### Pass 1 — Xi foundation and divisor representations (in progress)

Queries are exact declaration/module queries through `scripts/formal_tools.py research`; broad OR
search output is not used as evidence without exact follow-up.

| Mechanism | Carrier | Shadow map | Lost kernel | Shared invariant | Lift obstruction | Verdict |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| Centered Xi | entire source function `XiC` | zero divisor / evaluation | nonzero values and all relations not encoded by zero locus | evenness, Schwarz reality, order-one growth | reconstruct source normalization and pre-zero law from divisor | FOUNDATION |
| Hadamard product | `XiC` plus growth | `XiC_eq_constant_mul_canonicalProduct` | everything beyond multiplicity multiset and one constant | reflection involution, multiplicity, genus-one convergence | canonical product has no ordering, incidence, transition, or interaction data | SHADOW SINK |
| Deninger arithmetic jet determinant | source-jet generator | `deningerArithmeticJetRegularizedDeterminant_eq_xiCanonicalProduct` | nilpotent Jordan terms disappear by triangularity | same canonical product | lift determinant back to generator with arithmetic off-diagonal/Jordan law | TERMINAL SHADOW |
| Hedenmalm reciprocal Fredholm model | reciprocal operator | `fredholmDet_xiReciprocalDiagonalKernel_eq_xiCanonicalProduct` | operator realization beyond determinant | same canonical product | determinant does not reconstruct operator or its domain | TERMINAL SHADOW |
| CCM unitary polar atlas | polar support, residues, critical Bohr masses | `xiUnitaryPolarAtlasCanonicalProduct_eq` | atlas transition data after divisor reconstruction | same canonical product | reconstruction already receives divisor support/residues | DOWNSTREAM SHADOW |
| Weil polarization | divisor evaluation sequences | `xiWeilPolarization` and source comparison theorems | multiples of `XiC`; cross-orbit incidence | conjugation pairing, multiplicity, reflection parity | source packet representation cannot be recovered from scalar form | BLOCK-DIAGONAL SHADOW |

#### Exact findings

1. `XiEntireReal` supplies only global one-function symmetries: `XiC_even`, `XiC_conj`, entire
   regularity, and order-one growth. These constrain the divisor orbitwise but do not couple
   distinct conjugation orbits.
2. `XiHadamardGrowth` proves unconditional equality of `XiC` with a constant times the exact
   canonical product. Many named programmes therefore land on one scalar object, not independent
   explanatory structures.
3. `DeningerArithmeticJetDeterminant` documents decisive projection loss: nilpotent Jordan terms
   vanish under triangular determinant. Any global law carried by those terms is invisible to Xi.
4. `XiWeilPolarization` has exact radical invariance under adding a multiple of `XiC`.
   `GaussHodgeWeilPolarizationFusion` proves Hedenmalm eigen-Gram values vanish between distinct
   conjugation folds and couple only `alpha` to `conj alpha`. Thus the mature Weil shadow is a
   direct sum of orbit blocks, not a globally coupled divisor mechanism.
5. Functional-equation reflection preserves multiplicity and makes the canonical product even by
   reindexing. Reflection is shared across routes, but it remains an involutive orbit symmetry;
   it does not provide interaction among different heights.

#### Pass-1 survivor

Search above determinant and Weil scalarization. Candidate parent must retain noncommuting
off-diagonal/Jordan/transition information that:

- is source-defined before any Xi zero is selected;
- couples distinct height orbits rather than only functional-equation quartets;
- maps to all known scalar shadows after trace/determinant/evaluation;
- has an off-line obstruction living in its assembly law, even though every individual scalar
  shadow admits off-line parent models.

This is a structural direction, not yet a conjecture. Next foundation subpass audits every existing
operator/source realization of `XiC` to determine whether any already retains such pre-determinant
data or whether each is constructed from the divisor after the fact.

#### Fixed theta-source operator subpass

`HedenmalmThetaSourceDeterminantIdentity` is a genuine exception to the divisor-fed operator
models: it constructs one fixed Hilbert--Schmidt source inverse `B = thetaKernelOperator` from the
theta source and proves

```text
det₂(I + z B) = XiC(z) / XiC(0)
```

globally. `HedenmalmThetaSourceCompressedInverseDeterminant` then identifies nonzero spectrum of
`B` with reciprocal centered Xi divisor, including algebraic multiplicity. This is source-first,
but spectral reality of `B` is itself exactly RH.

The important information lies before determinant:

- canonical singular metric `G`;
- exact Green identity `G B - B† G = i C`;
- universal boundary operator `C` of exact rank two and mixed inertia `(1,1)`;
- generalized eigenvectors and Jordan chains;
- cross-polarized law on two root modes, where boundary pairing equals source-metric pairing times
  a spectral difference.

The corpus also closes the naive use of this structure.

- `HedenmalmThetaCompactFluxEnergy` squares individual boundary outputs and obtains an RH-equivalent
  global energy: scalar endpoint again.
- `HedenmalmThetaGreenHyperbolicGaugeNoGo` identifies the same rank-two current with Route R's split
  Hermitian current and proves gauge-invariant neutrality cannot select neutral grade.
- `HedenmalmThetaPrimeRankTwoCorrectionNoGo` proves every reflection-symmetric Hermitian correction
  generated by the pinned rank-two form vanishes on actual line-conjugate pairs and cannot repair
  the forward energy.

**Classification:** the fixed theta operator is a richer shadow than the canonical product, but a
single operator plus its rank-two Green current is still a named-route shadow. Its determinant loses
metric, boundary, Jordan, and transition data; its boundary scalarization reduces to known RH
energies. The next parent candidate must be a **diagram of source operators and transition laws**,
not `B` alone. Potential information-bearing location: compatibility of `B` under prime,
translation, scale, or source-decomposition morphisms before determinant and before boundary-flux
squaring.

`DeningerEntireQuotientCohomology` does not supply a second independent parent. Its own declaration
documents `DeningerEntireH1` as constructed without indexing zeros but **not independently of
`XiC`**: it is the entire-function algebra modulo the principal Xi ideal. It retains the exact
Jordan recurrence and reconstructs Xi point spectrum, but those are algebraic consequences of
quotienting by the target function. Classification: divisor-free notation, target-fed object,
DOWNSTREAM SHADOW.

### Pass 2 — explicit-formula source currents and finite-stage extinction

| Mechanism | Carrier | Shadow map | Lost kernel | Shared invariant | Lift obstruction | Verdict |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| Prime--Gamma relative trace | continued von Mangoldt plus archimedean current | equality with `XiC'/XiC` | multiplicative Euler incidence, source presentation, entire exact currents | meromorphic current and residues | lift abelian logarithmic current to coherent infinite factorization | SOURCE SHADOW |
| Guinand--Weil test algebra | finite Cauchy superpositions | linear source functional / divisor sum | nonlinear and higher interaction among tests and primes | relative resolvent | finite linear tests cannot see common blind packets | LINEAR SHADOW |
| Rectangle contours | source boundary integral | residue packet / power moments | all data modulo holomorphic exact terms; relations outside rectangle | integral charge and finite divisor multiset | glue compatible local moments without reverting to divisor Torelli | DOWNSTREAM |
| Central source germ | Taylor germ of normalized prime--Gamma trace | reciprocal moments / Hankel matrix | global continuation and source factorization | all scalar moment routes | positivity is already RH endpoint | ENDPOINT |
| Finite Euler/cumulant stages | entire finite source | derivative / contour charge | global monodromy absent at every finite stage | finite arithmetic labels | charge appears only through nonuniform completion | POSITIVE NO-GO |
| Completed SEM boundary | integral contour index | dyadic/additive charges | rational scalar extension kills class | integer winding / divisor order | retain integral or profinite class through completion | PRO-TOPOLOGICAL SHADOW |

#### Exact findings

1. `continuedPrimeArchSourceRelativeTrace` is genuinely source-defined, but
   `continuedPrimeArchSourceRelativeTrace_eq_xiRelativeResolventTrace` identifies it with one
   abelian logarithmic current. Prime multiplication has already become addition; commutators,
   factorization incidence, and higher coherence are absent.
2. `XiGuinandWeilExplicitFormula` is linear in finite Cauchy probes. `XiArithmeticSourceMomentTorelli`
   proves finite contour moments reconstruct the enclosed divisor exactly. This establishes
   faithfulness to the terminal multiset, not a production mechanism.
3. `PrimeBoundaryMonodromyEscape.circleIntegral_deriv_of_entire_eq_zero`: every finite cumulant is
   entire and has zero monodromy. `no_zero_of_tendstoUniformlyOn_zeroFree` makes the stronger point:
   a locally uniform zero-free finite-stage limit cannot acquire an enclosed zero.
4. `XiMassCriticalPrimeTailFiniteExtinction`: every fixed finite prime-power current becomes extinct
   at future height, while the assertion that one finite subtraction leaves a uniformly bounded
   infinite remainder is exactly RH. Under a nonzero reflection charge, every finite subtraction
   leaves an unbounded tail.
5. `XiLogDerivFiniteReporterBlindness`: every finite-dimensional reporter of the Xi
   logarithmic-derivative clock misses a genuine nonzero finite derivative packet; finite Gibbs
   dressing does not repair blindness.
6. `CompletedSEMContourBoundary`: the source-pure completed finite member carries an integral
   boundary index, but every additive finite charge kills it after rational scalar extension.
   Annihilation capable of seeing the obstruction must retain integral/torsion information.

#### Pass-2 survivor: completion defect

Divisor charge is absent at every finite Euler/cumulant stage and appears only after an infinite
completion where locally uniform zero-free convergence fails or boundary data escape. This points
above ordinary source functions to a pro-object and its failure of exact completion:

```text
finite arithmetic stages (charge 0)
          ↓ inverse/direct system with transition data
completed source (integral monodromy may be nonzero)
          ↓ complex logarithmic derivative / determinant / trace
Xi divisor shadows
```

Working parent candidate: an **integral arithmetic pro-descent object** whose derived completion
class records the obstruction to commuting finite-stage factorization, completion, and local
boundary extraction. A `lim¹`-type, determinant-line, or higher-cocycle class could be invisible in
every finite chart and under rational scalarization while remaining visible globally.

This is not yet a theorem or field name. It becomes viable only if later passes find compatible
transition data across currently unrelated source constructions and if the corpus's gluing/no-go
theorems do not already collapse it to a renamed contour charge.

### Pass 3 — Weil, Hankel, Li, and Laguerre--Polya shadows

| Shadow | Scalarization | What complete family retains | What each finite member loses | Verdict |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| Weil | divisor evaluation polarization | complete conjugation-block Krein form | every other orbit and all source presentation | RH ENDPOINT |
| Central Hankel | reciprocal-moment quadratic forms/determinants | full moment cone, hence RH | higher moments and non-Hankel incidence | RH ENDPOINT |
| Prime--Gamma Hankel | Taylor coefficients of normalized source germ | exactly central Xi Hankel hierarchy | global source continuation/factorization | SAME ENDPOINT |
| Li | traces of Cayley powers | full paired spectral multiset | phase distribution beyond selected power | RH ENDPOINT |
| Laguerre--Polya | locally uniform real-split polynomial approximation | real-zero entire class | source mechanism producing approximants | DOWNSTREAM |
| Jensen | finite coefficient windows | all windows encode classical Pólya criterion | every untested window | EXTERNAL DUPLICATE |

#### Exact findings

1. `XiWeilPositivityCriterion` detects one nonreal conjugate pair with a finitely supported sign
   test. It proves separability of violations, not global causation.
2. `XiCentralHankelDeterminantCriterion` compresses RH to one strict leading determinant in each
   degree. `PrimeArchHankelDeterminantCriterion` identifies the source Taylor hierarchy entrywise
   with the same matrices. Source notation changes no information.
3. `XiCountableHankelTestSetUniversalityLaw` is a decisive anti-complementarity theorem: countable,
   cofinal, sparse-degree, irreducible, and full Hermite presentations succeed or fail
   simultaneously. Mixing these charts cannot reveal a missing parent law.
4. `XiLaguerrePolya.xi_laguerrePolya_of_rh` constructs real-split approximants from the
   reflection-closed real divisor under RH. It is a receiver theorem, not a source approximation.
5. `HankelGuardrailCounterexamples` proves entrywise positivity, positive coefficients, all-order
   log-convexity, analytic complete monotonicity, unshifted Hankel positivity, and shifted Hankel
   positivity do not substitute for the full cone. At each finite order, shifted and unshifted
   barriers are logically independent; universal quantification is what restores force.

#### Pass-3 conclusion

No scalar test atlas inside a commutative moment/positivity framework can realize the requested
complementarity. Finite members are weak because they forget tail coordinates; complete families
recover RH because they reconstruct the same terminal commutative object. The unknown parent must
carry relations **between coefficient degrees/tests before taking quadratic forms or traces**.

Potentially relevant contrast: Pass 2's integral completion defect is destroyed by rational scalar
extension, whereas every Pass-3 criterion lives over `ℝ` or `ℂ`. This strongly separates the
candidate parent class from positivity theory.

### Pass 4 — named operator programmes as shadow functors

#### CCM

- `CCMUnconditionalFoundation` identifies global Weil passivity, rank-two regular Cauchy
  passivity, continued prime--archimedean passivity, and cofinal source-resolved Fock
  contractivity. These are equivalent scalar consumers, not independent producers.
- `CCMClosedXiHaarMellinRealization` attaches support functionals to actual Xi support and its
  closed quotient pairing is exactly Xi Weil polarization; Clifford phases cancel before
  numerical evaluation.
- `CCMUnitaryPolarSourceAtlas` first classifies actual Xi zero copies into strict-right, critical,
  and reflected charts, then reconstructs the canonical product. It is a complete downstream
  divisor atlas.
- `CCMEvansDeterminantLine` exposes a genuine pre-scalar loss: sign reversal changes the Plücker
  vector while the Gram determinant is unchanged. The relevant top exterior target can have
  dimension greater than one, so there is no canonical scalar determinant line; distinct scalar
  extractions disagree. **Survivor:** retain oriented/projective Plücker vectors and their
  transitions before Gram/determinant scalarization.
- `CCMConnesConsaniScalingClosure` defines the closed source by Fourier vanishing at the already
  sampled critical-line zero set. It is target-fed.

#### Deninger

- `DeningerArithmeticPoincareDuality` supplies separating Poincare pairing and weight-one cup
  derivation; the missing Hodge-star positivity is the whole RH-strength endpoint.
- `DeningerClosedOperatorPolarization` shows a polarized closed generator has critical-line
  spectrum and no Jordan chains, but independently constructing that generator/polarization is
  absent.
- `DeningerArithmeticResidueFlowTotalization` gives a useful generic pre-shadow: a two-term
  mapping-fibre total complex whose middle cocycles are exactly agreements of residue and flow
  faces; `D₁ D₀ = 0` is residue naturality. The actual arithmetic-Xi comparison is missing.
- Arithmetic jet determinants, divisor closed generators, and entire-function quotient
  cohomology are target-fed by Xi/divisor data. Local sheaf infrastructure does not solve the
  non-quasi-compact/infinite descent problem.

#### Suzuki / de Branges / resolvent

- `SuzukiDirichletFormCore` identifies the literal Sobolev core densely with the restricted CCM
  Weil form domain. It is not a separate parent route.
- `SuzukiLagariasMeromorphicInner` makes the shifted-Xi quotient meromorphic inner exactly on the
  corresponding zero-free half-plane family: a scalar endpoint.
- `SuzukiCanonicalHankelOperator` constructs the global Hankel unitary as
  `F⁻¹ M_Theta R F`; `SuzukiCanonicalHankelCompressionBridge` identifies literal finite Hankel
  operators as compressions. `SuzukiCanonicalHankelLemma44` proves both signed finite resolvents
  invertible in the safe unconditional shift range. Thus failure enters at singular completion to
  the critical boundary, not at finite safe stages.
- `SuzukiAffineResolventKreinDefect` computes the exact local sign defect. A negative spectral node
  forces positive compensation debt; projective coupling adds a strict Schur holonomy tax; no
  squared-norm or `B*B` presentation exists. Local positivity cannot be the producer. The missing
  global/local weld must carry signed/projective data.
- `SuzukiDeBrangesDeficiencyKernel` builds the kernel from `E = XiC + i XiC'`; its spectral
  relation is a Xi quotient. It receives the completed target function.

#### Burnol / Sonin

- `BurnolCoPoissonGeneralSeedMellin` is source-first up to scalarization: every compact co-Poisson
  source has completed Mellin transform `zeta(w) * M(seed)(w)`. At a zeta zero, evaluation
  annihilates every source. This realizes a zero as failure of source detectability, not as a
  produced spectral point.
- `BurnolCoPoissonCosineClosedZeroJetDetection` proves the source annihilator condition is exactly
  the zeta zero set in the right strip. `BurnolCoPoissonGeneralSeedCompletedZeroJets` records
  multiplicity by simultaneous lower-jet annihilation.
- `burnolCoPoissonQuotientSpace` is the orthogonal defect space of extended Sonine vectors against
  all literal co-Poisson sources. It is a genuine source-defined error carrier. However its
  evaluation at a chosen spectral character remains the scalar shadow.
- `BurnolDividedShiftSourceLeakage` proves a nonzero source cannot be shifted entirely into the
  source orthogonal and gives a quantitative leakage floor at an actual right-half divisor. The
  proposed global source-flatness excluding that leakage is itself RH-strength.
- `BurnolBilateralApertureRH` computes unconditional positive holonomy on a right-half annihilator
  fibre; the missing nonpositive order on those fibres is again the endpoint.

#### Topological-defect hostile audit

- `QuadrantSeamConservation` proves local rung-one/parity charge is insufficient: symmetric
  off-seam islands can carry odd point charges while aggregate/seam charge is even or trivial.
- `AdelicPhaseDefectCharacterBlindnessLaw` is decisive. Coboundary defects are invariant under
  character twists, while the source-inverse character is trivial exactly on the critical line.
  Therefore no predicate on unframed phase-defect/cocycle data can detect horizontal grade.
- `XiSourceModularTypeSuperselectionLaw` shows one actual right-half Xi divisor carries the same
  positive modular charge across normal-cone, Hurwitz, shell, Sonin/Calkin, Ward, and Wold--Poisson
  consumers. This proves common-shadow saturation, not exclusion: the global neutrality predicate
  is exactly RH at divisor level.
- `ThetaCuspHawkingCausalHardyIndexEndpoint` identifies one resonance with one missing Hardy
  direction and equates defect index with winding, but absence of the extra inner defect is exactly
  RH-strength; lossless scattering permits the defect.

#### Pass-4 conclusion

Plain soliton/topological language fails. A viable error object must be a **framed or graded
completed defect**: integral charge/multiplicity plus a retained continuous modular character and
oriented projective transition data. The framing is essential because quotienting by characters,
taking Gram determinants, or aggregating reflection pairs erases exactly the horizontal grade.

Named routes then become different scalarizations of one error fibre. The remaining parent-law
question is sharper: what source-defined global framing makes every completed annihilator/error
class norm-conservative (hence zero real modular rate) without assuming its Xi support?

### Pass 5 — mechanism-first fringe audit

#### Hawking completion and partner memory

- `ThetaCuspHawkingTiltedBackreactionGaugeEndpoint` shows an off-line zero becomes a zero-frequency,
  zero-relative-charge, passivity-saturating mode in its own co-moving reflected horizon; its
  laboratory imbalance is modular work. Local horizon gauge therefore neutralizes every candidate
  defect. A useful law must keep one absolute arithmetic/source frame.
- `ThetaCuspHawkingModularBoostEndpoint` proves opposite source-ray aperture products are invariant,
  while serial reduced dynamics with a freshly reset partner cannot reproduce the coherent boost:
  an exact positive partner-memory defect remains. Scalar passivity of the observed Xi channel is
  RH-strength, but the non-Markovian memory is unconditional source data.

### Pass 7 — Noether lift of the error-packet picture

#### Candidate continuous symmetry

The natural connected symmetry is centered multiplicative dilation/modular time. A divisor packet
at `rho` carries the real character

`a |-> exp((Re rho - 1/2) a)`.

A fixed norm-conservative realization can carry a nonzero such packet only at zero real weight.
Functional-equation reflection is the additional discrete involution reversing the transverse
character, so the expected full symmetry is an `R`-flow equipped with a Real/reflection structure
(morally `R semidirect Z/2`), not reflection alone.

This makes the critical line the unitary sector of a continuous source symmetry. Zeros remain error
packets; Noether law constrains which modular weights a non-erased packet may carry.

#### Existing variational infrastructure

- `CyclicActionNoether.cyclic_action_euler_lagrange_noether` supplies a complete action,
  Euler--Lagrange/Maurer--Cartan identification, and Noether package at every finite quotient and in
  the separated inverse limit. `NoetherCorpusApplications.noether_cyclicAction_complete` exposes it
  as reusable corpus infrastructure.
- The BV/Hamiltonian master equation is exactly the square-zero source law. Second-Noether results
  give off-shell gauge kernels and nonabelian Bianchi identities. On the arithmetic side,
  truncating a finite exponential prime source exposes exactly one Gamma-clock curvature face.
- First-Noether applications include exact Xi residue/Cayley--Ward Poisson packets and a common
  closed orientation charge shared by nearby-cycle, divisor, and reflected-source currents.
- `ConstitutiveObserverFibreChirality` is particularly relevant: source information current splits
  exactly into visible current plus fibre current, composes through observer towers, and cannot be
  destroyed by terminal reversal—it localizes into hidden stage currents.

#### Strongest error-conservation evidence

- `MobiusGammaXiCoupledErrorDebtCancellationDichotomy` proves visible cancellation is exactly either
  RH or the unique universal controller that erases every scalar source value. Erasure does not
  remove the debt: balanced visible/hidden ports conserve energy, and the hidden port carries twice
  the original normalized debt. Removing the erasing branch leaves precisely RH.
- This is a formal instance of the desired ontology: a zero/error can become dark in one observer,
  but its debt must reappear in a complementary sector. The decisive selector is **non-erasure**,
  not positivity of the visible channel.

#### Noether hostile audit

- Noether is not automatic from the word “conservation.” A usable theorem needs a source-defined
  field space, action functional, continuous group action, regularity/variation, and identification
  of the charge. Converse-Noether reasoning is invalid without such structure.
- `RiemannCenteredFlowCapacityIntertwining` shows why. Raw complete order capacity transforms by the
  transverse character, but a zero-dependent prime-tail countercharacter trivializes it to conserved
  multiplicity mass at every divisor point. A co-moving/renormalized gauge can make every off-line
  defect look conserved. The action and metric must be fixed before selecting the zero and may not
  absorb its horizontal grade.
- `noetherObstruction_routeRFixedPolarEnergy` proves the tempting fixed positive quadratic energy is
  not gauge invariant; the invariant hyperbolic energy is not compressible to a quadratic charge.
  `ClockDynamicsUnderdetermination` likewise warns that conserved effective stress tensors can be
  manufactured from arbitrary geometric left sides.
- Bianchi identities conserve anomaly flow but do not annihilate anomaly. Coarse reflection
  holonomy can cancel while higher obstruction classes survive. Noether-II alone cannot prove RH.

#### Revised parent-law target

Seek a **source-first non-erasing Noether completion**:

1. fields are visible Xi/vacuum response plus involution-selected complementary records and ordered
   prime-interaction memory;
2. action is constructed from prime/Gamma source before divisor selection and survives the separated
   inverse limit;
3. centered dilation is a genuine continuous symmetry for one fixed positive or otherwise coercive
   physical structure, with reflection as Real symmetry;
4. Noether current splits exactly across visible and hidden sectors, so a scalar zero reroutes but
   never erases source information;
5. no zero-dependent countercharacter or universal opposite controller is allowed;
6. every completed Xi annihilator lands as a nonzero finite-action error packet.

Under these clauses, the existing source-character conservation theorem forces every packet to have
`Re rho = 1/2`. Novel work is clauses 2, 3, and 6—not the final one-line Noether deduction.

### Targeted external comparison — mechanisms, not RH routes

- [Generalised conservation laws in non-local field theories](https://arxiv.org/abs/1506.03320)
  proves that an action symmetry in a nonlocal theory yields a current with an additional nonlocal
  correction. This independently matches the corpus's visible-current plus fibre-current split:
  missing visible flux need not vanish; it can be the nonlocal correction.
- [Robustness of Noether's principle for quantum channels](https://arxiv.org/abs/1908.04254)
  quantifies the disconnect between covariance and conservation in open dynamics and relates the
  defect to unitarity and complementary channels. This validates the hostile requirement that the
  joint visible-plus-record completion, not the visible channel, must be closed and non-erasing.
- [Topological nature of bound states in the continuum](https://arxiv.org/abs/1408.0237) treats
  nonradiating dark states as vortex centers of the far-field polarization map with conserved integer
  winding and strict creation/annihilation rules. This is a much closer physical analogue for Xi
  zeros than a soliton: a zero can be a topological defect of the observation/radiation map.
- [Fusion Category Symmetry II](https://arxiv.org/abs/2106.12577) proves a Noether theorem relating
  continuous fusion-category symmetries, generated by noninvertible topological defect lines, to
  nonlocal conserved currents. [Fusion Category Symmetry I](https://arxiv.org/abs/1912.02817)
  organizes their fusion, crossings, boundary realizations, and anomaly inflow.

#### External novelty boundary

External work supports three ingredients separately—nonlocal Noether corrections, noninvertible
defect symmetries, and topological dark states—but supplies no arithmetic source action, prime-fusion
category, Xi annihilator landing, or critical-line theorem. Current synthesis is therefore not a
renaming of a known RH route. Working label: **arithmetic noninvertible Noether theory**.

In this proposed field, prime residue/valuation sectors are objects; convolution and collision give
fusion; continuation/observation maps are generally noninvertible defect operators; Xi zeros are
dark defects of the vacuum observer; complementary records are their nonlocal Noether current;
`lim^1`/cofinal Bockstein classes are anomalies of infinite completion; and centered dilation with
Real reflection supplies the continuous grading. Critical-line placement would say every physical,
non-erased completed defect belongs to the unitary fixed-grade sector.

### Frozen first target — shared-gauge reflection descent atlas

`[REDISCOVERED]` Three ingredients already exist separately: single-character conservative descent

`[PROVED]` **Shared-gauge reflection descent atlas.** Let `s` range over the open critical strip
away from the critical line and put `delta(s) = Re s - 1/2`. Use the actual complete positive
Riemann-resolution packets `V(s)` and `V(R s)`, where `R s = 1 - conjugate s`. A candidate observer

their transported observations. For a real rate `lambda`, define a local good package by

1. source observation is nonzero;
2. transport preserves its absolute norm;
3. transport intertwines multiplication by `exp lambda`.

The three producer obligations at `s` are

- `ForwardGood`: the local good package at effective rate `g+ + delta(s)` on `V(s)`;
- `ReflectedGood`: the local good package at effective rate `g- - delta(s)` on `V(R s)`;
- `SharedGauge`: `g+ = g-`, the overlap/descent condition.

The three failure charts are the negations of these obligations. They cover the entire off-line
strip for every candidate observer. Expanding either local failure gives the source-erasure,
metric-nonconservation, and modular-nonequivariance subcharts. No Xi zero is used in chart
definition.

`[PROVED]` **Paper proof of cover.** A nonzero norm-preserved observation intertwining the real
character `exp lambda` forces `exp lambda = 1`, hence `lambda = 0`. Therefore `ForwardGood` gives
`g+ = -delta(s)` and `ReflectedGood` gives `g- = delta(s)`. If `SharedGauge` also holds, then
`delta(s) = -delta(s)`, so `delta(s) = 0`, contradicting membership in the off-line strip. Hence
`not ForwardGood or not ReflectedGood or not SharedGauge` at every point.

`[PROVED]` **Minimality / strict local burden.** All three proper subfamilies have explicit
off-line models, using a nonzero scalar source observation and identity transport:

- forward + reflected: `g+ = -delta`, `g- = delta`; both local laws hold and the overlap gauge
  splits;
- forward + shared: `g+ = g- = -delta`; forward law holds and reflected covariance fails;
- reflected + shared: `g+ = g- = delta`; reflected law holds and forward covariance fails.

Thus no pair of producer obligations forces neutral grade in the parent character model. Only the
three-way compatibility does. This is stronger than showing each obligation separately weak.

## Level correction: shared gauge is a shadow, not the universal open core

`[RETIRED AS UNIVERSAL TARGET]` The preceding final sentence named construction of one fixed
prime/Gamma action with a shared reflection gauge as the scientific open core.  That is one valid
atlas test after choosing an observer, but it is not the containing law.  Action, Noether current,
gauge, passivity, topology, spectrality, and positivity are different representations of a defect
which exists before any of those choices.

The useful statement survives in corrected form:

> Off-line divisor points are failures of globally consistent reflection descent, not locally
> generated objects.

The corpus now separates two superficially similar dagger statements which must not be conflated.
The pre-divisor prime phase has unit modulus and its scalar dagger defect vanishes unconditionally.
The completed source-dagger identity, however, asks the resolved source shear to commute with the
completed-Xi Hodge rotation on one retained coefficient fibre; at any fixed ordinary prime this is
equivalent to the critical line.  Source reversal exists.  Completion/landing can fail to transport
it conservatively.

## Whole-corpus quotient: one defect, many observers

For a divisor point `rho` of order `m`, the signed affine quantity

```text
-(rho - 1/2) * m
```

is already carried, without RH, by multiple independently constructed organs:

| Organ | Exact shadow |
|---|---|
| bounded-exponent normal cone | residue of exceptional section `1 / Xi` |
| depth nearby cycle | outer depth-successor charge |
| reflected prime tail | source entropy / transverse horizon rate |
| Hurwitz chart | principal monodromy phase |
| Gamma first-trace chart | signed cofinal Bockstein / determinant transgression |
| source-dagger cyclic chart | paracyclic monodromy gain |
| Hawking / recurrence charts | horizon Lyapunov mode / collision hierarchy |
| positive spectral charts | squared orientation, Hodge, or Fisher energy |

Thus these routes do not describe different candidate causes.  They read one completion defect
after different information-erasing operations.  Even/positive observers erase orientation;
bounded observers erase cofinal escape; ordinary completion collapses source points; finite Rees
operators erase essential sewing type; scalar total charge lets reflected pairs cancel.

The canonical geometric carrier is the projectivized normal cone / exceptional boundary of
arithmetic completion.  Every Xi zero is a pole of the exceptional reciprocal section and hence a
completion defect in a literal sense.  The whole exceptional boundary is not confined to the
critical line: the two-prime blow-up chart faithfully parametrizes arbitrary spectral points.
Only singular defect support, not the ambient boundary, can be confined.

## Sharper candidate: subquantum arithmetic defect confinement

`[CONJECTURE -- NOT RH PROOF]` A completed arithmetic doctrine change which contains no new source
intervention cannot amplify intervention-generated defect capacity.  Equivalently, the singular
completion comparison is nonamplifying for the source's integral collision/BPS index even when it
is unbounded, nonclosable, or only defined on a projective/derived normal-cone graph.

This law is about errors, not producers:

```text
different microscopic prime histories
    -> different exceptional-boundary defects
    -> one universal off-wall capacity quantum
    -> source completion has less than one quantum available
    -> no defect can leave the reflection fixed wall.
```

The quantization is exact but subtler than quantizing horizontal displacement.  The displacement
`Re rho - 1/2` is continuous.  Off-line existence is quantized because functional-equation
reflection creates a distinct same-height partner with the same positive integral multiplicity.
Consequently every nonempty off-line window has horizontal collision charge at least `2`.
Critical-line zeros remain exceptional-boundary defects but carry zero transverse collision
charge.

The corpus already proves three sides of a subquantum exclusion pattern in several normalizations:

1. **Integral endpoint:** `xiHorizontalCollisionCharge` is monotone, integer-valued, and any
   off-line zero forces at least the two-unit reflected-pair quantum.
2. **BPS/exterior endpoint:** every off-line support point carries a uniform two-orientation
   Ward--Green exterior quantum, with multiplicity-square packet lower bound.
3. **Source capacity:** the complete natural-number von-Mangoldt prime--Gamma cubic trace equals
   `routePPrimitiveCollisionCubicTraceBudget`, and a symbolic proof gives
   `routePPrimitiveCollisionCubicTraceBudget < 115 / 108`, below the least cubic charge of one
   strict-right zero.

The missing statement is not another detector and not a mechanism creating zeros.  It is the
universal middle inequality: the completed physical residue/collision capacity cannot exceed the
literal pre-divisor prime--Gamma capacity.  In one exact cubic chart this is the still-unproved
global contraction from the total reflection-Loewner residue trace to the primitive Euler source
trace.  Other routes seek shadows of the same inequality as passivity, source-dagger survival,
nonexpansive Green landing, subunit Hodge curvature, shared gauge, finite-grade recovery, or
bounded collision history.

The proposed containing principle is therefore:

> **Arithmetic defect-capacity confinement.** Source completion may move, hide, or singularize an
> integral defect class, but a doctrine change without a new source intervention cannot increase
> its absolute capacity across the first nonzero topological quantum.

This is compatible with every zero having a different microscopic reason.  RH needs no common
microscopic cause.  It needs only the global rule that none of those errors can pay the cost of a
transverse reflected pair.

## First non-theatrical construction target

Construct one pre-divisor constitutive response square, not a zero-indexed action:

1. horizontal operation: insert/refine a literal prime or exponent-depth source cell;
2. vertical operation: Möbius/Gamma completion and reflection doctrine change;
3. exchange response: the unsquared prime--Gamma Bockstein / cubic Loewner current on the source
   normal cone;
4. source capacity: its natural-number von-Mangoldt cubic trace;
5. physical capacity: its coefficient-faithful residue trace before scalar fusion.

Prove that the comparison is capacity-nonincreasing for a source-generated reason which survives
cofinal graph completion.  Finite positivity, ordinary bounded-map contractivity, and a fitted
completely positive channel are forbidden substitutes: faithful recovery is already known to be
singular, and choosing the comparison after divisor specialization is RH in disguise.

If this law is proved, the subquantum source bound excludes even one right-half defect; reflection
then gives RH.  If it fails, the positive survivor is equally sharp: analytic completion is an
active law intervention, not a passive observer, and its newly created capacity is the sought
universal anomaly.

## Correction: the universal law must force the shared gauge

`[SUPERSEDES THE PRECEDING LEVEL CORRECTION AND GENERIC CAPACITY CONJECTURE]` The statement that a
fixed prime--Gamma action with one reflected gauge is merely an observer-level shadow was a level

Searching for another fitted action or reporter is equally wrong: the corpus already classifies
local repairs and proves that they exist at every horizontal rate.  The universal theorem must
force one pre-divisor action to be insensitive to different presentations of one arithmetic
source class.  Noether descent then forces the shared gauge.

The proposed generic defect-capacity inequality is also not valid as stated.
`SourceHorizontalSharedReservoirAmplification` constructs source-labelled, prime-injective,

nonexpansive physical landing.  `GammaCellMobility` and `GammaCellNonrealMobility` show Gamma
completion can carry off-wall zeros without a real-strip obstruction.  Capacity bounds can be
consequences of a stronger source-objectivity law, but cannot be that law.

## Candidate universal law: arithmetic source equivalence under pure completion

`[BOLD CONJECTURE -- NOT RH PROOF]` Let the literal integral Euler--Gamma/co-Poisson source be
formed before divisor specialization.  Prime-labelled and archimedean orientation cochains which
represent the same retained source class are physical gauge presentations of one event.  A
completion which introduces no new source intervention must preserve that equivalence in its
complete bidirectional source record.  Therefore every current obtained from one fixed completed
action factors through the retained source class and annihilates presentation-change boundaries.
Reflection is the reverse descent of that same action, so its forward and reflected local
Noether descents use one global gauge.

In compressed form:

```text
same arithmetic source class
  -> same complete bidirectional source record
  -> same action value and Noether response
  -> one reflection-overlap gauge
  -> zero transverse character
```

This is an arithmetic equivalence principle for completion.  Its negative form describes the
zeros as errors: an off-line divisor point is a completion anomaly which assigns different
physical responses to homologous presentations of one source event.  Different zeros may have
different microscopic prime histories, but all violate the same macroscopic objectivity law.

### Exact RH implication already present in the corpus

For every ordinary prime `p`, the explicit Calderon--Witt source complex has a Green primitive
`G_p` with

```text
e_p - e_infinity = boundary(G_p),
```

where `e_p` and `e_infinity` are prime and archimedean presentations of the same retained top
class.  On a horizontal rate `t`, the already-classified inverse-oriented prime--Gamma Ward law
forces the unique positive local response to be `p^(-t)`.  If `J_t` is the current of the fixed
source action, pure completion gives

```text
J_t(boundary(G_p)) = 0,
```

and hence `J_t(e_p) = J_t(e_infinity) = c`, with one prime-independent archimedean value `c`.
Applying this at primes two and three gives

```text
2^(-t) = c = 3^(-t),
```

so `t = 0`.  On an Xi conormal fibre, `t = 2 * Re(rho) - 1`; therefore `Re(rho) = 1/2`.
`SourceGammaNaturalitySharedGhostProducer.lean` formalizes every implication after boundary
naturality is supplied.  `CompleteResolutionSharedGaugeReflectionAtlas.lean` independently proves
that forward covariance, reflected covariance, and one overlap gauge are jointly impossible
off-line and that every two-condition face is realizable.  Thus the proposed law lands on the
exact sharp obstruction, not a weaker RH receiver.

No action is chosen at `rho`.  The action, source complex, Green primitives, Gamma orientation,
and prime coefficients are fixed before divisor reading; a zero only selects a retained conormal
fibre on which the universal law is tested.

### Whole-corpus quotient

| Corpus family | Shadow of arithmetic source equivalence under pure completion |
|---|---|
| complete-resolution/shared-gauge atlas | local forward and reflected laws must descend from one overlap gauge |
| Deninger/Calderon--Witt/ABHN | local prime and archimedean presentations belong to one global source class; reciprocity is global consistency |
| Connes/cyclic/Noether/Bockstein | action-derived current must vanish on gauge boundaries; nonzero transgression is the completion anomaly |
| source-record hyperrigidity | complementary dilation record prevents completion from silently splitting one source class into inequivalent physical sectors |
| arithmetic objectivity/recovery | forward continuation and reflected descent are encoder and recovery of one logical conormal event |
| Hawking/KMS/Bogoliubov | partner channels are two descents of one source interaction; a common clock is shared-gauge descent, while delayed onset is presentation dependence |
| quantum recurrence/formation history | all finite histories must form one compatible bidirectional Pro--Ind record; finite agreement cannot hide a cofinal gauge split |
| Hedenmalm/first-trace/Suzuki/Sonin | singular boundary and Hardy defects retain the presentation anomaly erased by ordinary Hilbert completion |
| Weil/positive/Hodge/spectral routes | positivity and unitarity are consequences after source-class descent; they square or forget the unsquared orientation defect when imposed first |

The unsquared two-prime holonomy is

```text
log(2^(-t)) - log(3^(-t)) = t * (log 3 - log 2).
```

This explains why two primes suffice, why one-prime corrections are fitted and nonselective, and
why positive squared energies see only a shadow of the primary defect.

### Hostile audit

2. **Not generic completion.** Cofinal completion can create boundary classes from finite exact

3. **Not generic Noether/Bianchi.** An anomalous current may fail to annihilate gauge directions,

   is prime-presentation independence of one action-derived current.
5. **Not positivity or contractivity.** Positive completion can erase orientation, and arbitrary
   recovery channels can be fitted after divisor specialization.  Source-defined exact reverse

   Literal integer source relations and their complete anomaly record are load-bearing.
7. **Not finite exactness.** Every finite source gauge may be exact while a nonzero cofinal
   anomaly survives.  Compatibility must include all prime-power, depth, Mertens-jet, and
   exponentially flat source grades.

Prove a source-relative completion-purity theorem, independent of Xi zeros:

Applied to the explicit prime--archimedean Green boundaries, this theorem constructs no fitted

## Falsification: presentation equality is not arithmetic objectivity

`[FALSIFIED — SUPERSEDES "Candidate universal law: arithmetic source equivalence under pure
completion"]` The preceding two-prime argument made a categorical mistake.  The values
`p^(-t)` at different ordinary primes need not be equal in order to arise from one global law.
They are the prime restrictions of the single completely multiplicative character

```text
n |-> n^(-t).
```

Thus `2^(-t) = 3^(-t)` is not forced merely because the two local responses belong to one source
class or one fixed action.  It is already an RH-strength extra condition.  The Meyer prime-time
formalization makes the correct structure explicit: the fixed arithmetic flow has character
`p^(rho - 1/2)` at prime `p`, and the prime dependence is coherent rather than anomalous.
Presentation independence must preserve this global character, not collapse its local values.

The useful survivor of the rejected candidate is narrower.  Prime and reflected presentations
must be related by one source-defined recovery law, fixed before any zero is read.  What is
forbidden is not prime-dependent value, but irreversible norm change of the retained logical
source event under a purportedly exact forward/reflected passage.

## Replacement candidate: dagger-conservative arithmetic objectivity

`[BOLD CONJECTURE — NOT RH PROOF]` The literal pre-divisor Euler--Gamma/co-Poisson source admits a
completed source-record representation with normalized prime transports `T_p` and reflected
recoveries `R_p`, defined without reference to a divisor point, such that:

1. each actual Xi principal conormal maps to a nonzero logical source section;
2. `T_p` and `R_p` preserve the source order/unit structure and are dagger compatible;
3. on the retained logical source sector, `R_p T_p = id` and `T_p R_p = id`;
4. finite-prime/depth realizations form one compatible cofinal system, and the inverse-limit map
   remains faithful on every actual Xi conormal;
5. the existing source-normal transport law acts on the conormal selected by `rho` through the
   already-proved eigencharacter `p^(rho - 1/2)` (up to the corpus's chosen reciprocal
   orientation).

This law does not produce a zero and does not identify different primes.  It says an actual zero
is a retained source event, while completion plus reflection is exact reversible transport of
that event.  Exact positive reversible transport is an order isomorphism; in a C*-realization,
mutually inverse unital completely positive maps are multiplicative on the recovered sector.
Dagger conservation then makes the scalar eigencharacter unitary:

```text
|p^(rho - 1/2)| = 1.
```

For any ordinary prime `p > 1`, this gives `Re(rho) = 1/2`.  Different zeros may have completely
different formation histories; all that is universal is that an actual, recoverable arithmetic
event cannot acquire or lose norm when carried around the completion/reflection loop.  Off-line
zeros are therefore global completion errors: retained source events whose reflected recovery
would have nonunit holonomy.

### Why this is not another classic producer search

Prime character, Gamma/reflection transfer, actual Xi conormal, and abstract inverse-UCP rigidity
already exist in the formal corpus.  Unknown is not an operator with prescribed spectrum and not
a positivity criterion fitted at `rho`.  It is a source theorem saying literal arithmetic
completion is an exact reversible channel on the logical conormal sector it itself declares
actual.  Spectral, Hodge, Hawking, recurrence, BV, ABHN, and shared-gauge routes become different
observations of the same reversibility constraint:

| shared forward/reflected gauge | one transport and its dagger inverse |

| recurrence / complete formation history | cofinal recovery, not merely finite-stage return |

| Bockstein / BV anomaly | obstruction to exact recovery on retained conormal |

### Hostile audit of the replacement

1. **Pre-divisor dagger is too weak.** `RouteRPrimeLogEssentialCapFixedGradePhaseDagger` proves a

3. **Finite-stage inverses are insufficient.** A nonzero anomaly can enter through cofinal

4. **A fitted channel is circular.** `T_p`, `R_p`, operator systems, and dagger must be constructed

5. **Actuality-to-correctability is hard claim.** Nonzero source incidence does not generally
   imply exact recovery.  This implication needs a new arithmetic reason; it cannot be promoted

6. **Cofinal bounded recovery may be false.** Existing arithmetic-clock results show norms of
   bounded recoveries can diverge.  Correct category may require a closed unbounded source-dagger
   correspondence rather than a bounded Hilbert-space channel, but it must still yield an
   isometry on each actual logical conormal.
7. **Generalized-prime falsifier.** If an off-line generalized-prime system satisfies same source
   axioms and admits same recovery, conjecture is false.  Literal integer divisibility, integral
   Cartier structure, and archimedean self-dual record must be shown load-bearing rather than
   listed decoratively.

### Exact open core after correction

Construct, before divisor specialization, a cofinal source correspondence

```text
literal Euler--Gamma source  --T_p-->  completed/reflected source record
literal Euler--Gamma source  <--R_p--  completed/reflected source record
```

and prove all three non-circular facts:

1. **source reversibility:** `R_p T_p = id` on the integral logical conormal module;
2. **cofinal faithfulness:** the map from every actual Xi principal conormal into that module is
   nonzero and survives inverse-limit completion;
3. **dagger compatibility:** reflected transport is adjoint recovery for source norm/order coming
   from literal arithmetic action.

Then formal consumer is short: actual Xi conormal `x_rho != 0`,
`T_p x_rho = p^(rho-1/2) x_rho`, exact dagger recovery makes `T_p` isometric on `x_rho`, hence
`|p^(rho-1/2)| = 1`, hence `Re(rho)=1/2`.  Until three source statements are proved without Xi
line information, RH is not proved.

## Source-marked Lawson completion audit

`[BOLD CONJECTURE — FORMAL INFRASTRUCTURE BUILT, RH NOT PROVED]`

The previous completion candidates still allowed a moving-generator cheat: stage `N` could kill a
different target vector from stage `N + 1`, while ordinary pointwise, strong, weak, or product
convergence forgot that every vector came from one literal pre-divisor boundary.  The correct
object must retain the source marker while the target completion varies.

### New formal infrastructure

The following axiom-free Lean modules were added and imported by their theorem-area umbrellas:

- `HTGeometry/CompletionDefectExactnessFlag.lean`: boundary exactness flags, escape classes,
  survival, and closed exactness limits;
- `HTGeometry/ResolutionExactnessProfile.lean`: sharp exactness wall with a nonnegative core radius;
- `HTGeometry/ProvenanceContinuousCompletion.lean`: pointwise provenance convergence criterion;
- `HTGeometry/SourceMarkedExactnessCompletion.lean`: target exactness flag plus a fixed source
  landing; source escape-freedom is closed;
- `HTGeometry/SourceMarkedLawsonCompletion.lean`: two-sided lower--Scott topology on exact source
  profiles; every fixed marker's exact/surviving phase is clopen;
- `RHTower/RouteRCompletionMigrationDefect.lean`: the literal Fisher source boundary and its
  fixed-grade Roos escape class;
- `RHTower/RouteRResolutionCoreDefect.lean`: physical core radius
  `|2 * Re(rho) - 1|`;
- `RHTower/RouteRResolutionCoreNoetherCharge.lean`: exact Noether charge/core calibration;
- `RHTower/RouteRSourceMarkedFiniteCompletion.lean`: canonical finite exact stages and the sharp
  source-marked completion wall.

Key generic object:

```text
state = (target boundary/cycle flag, fixed-source linear landing)
exactSourceProfile(state) = landing^-1(target boundary).
```

`SourceMarkedLawsonCompletion.lean` proves that its topology is the pullback of the join of:

```text
lower topology: x notin exactSourceProfile     -- defect survives
Scott topology: x in exactSourceProfile        -- source relation is exact.
```

Hence a net converges exactly when every literal source marker eventually has its limiting
homological status.  This is the missing bridge to `InterfinityGeometry`: its stable death regions
are the Scott half; provenance survival is the lower half.  Joining them gives a Lawson-style
moduli geometry of exact source relations.

### Concrete finite-to-physical firewall

For a trace sequence `a`, define its literal finite head

```text
Tr_N(a)(n) = if n < N then a(n) else 0.
```

Lean proves, without any Xi placement hypothesis:

1. every `Tr_N(a)` is summable;
2. its diagonal tail mismatch is a Roos boundary;
3. every finite state is escape-free on the entire source line generated by the literal Fisher
   boundary;
4. at each fixed target coordinate, finite and physical landings eventually agree exactly.

But it also proves the sharp separation

```text
finite exact states --> physical state in source-marked topology
    iff t = 0,
```

for `|t| < 1`, and therefore on Xi support

```text
finite exact states --> physical state in source-marked topology
    iff Re(rho) = 1/2.
```

The same equivalence holds for the two-sided Lawson topology, and universal Lawson convergence on
all Xi support is equivalent to RH.  Thus ordinary coordinate convergence is now formally ruled
out as a proof mechanism: it holds unconditionally and still permits a nonzero `lim^1` completion
class.  Fixed-source homological convergence is the exact missing datum.

### Soliton/defect split

The zeros can be solitonic without their horizontal displacement being an allowed soliton charge.
The formal corpus now separates two invariants:

```text
allowed topological charge       = multiplicity / winding / Fredholm index;
forbidden transverse anomaly     = horizontal completion charge;
resolution core radius           = |2 * Re(rho) - 1|;
|Noether orientation charge|     = multiplicity / 2 * core radius.
```

Hawking causal Hardy theory supplies the first shadow: one resonance has a one-dimensional defect
and one Wigner--Smith winding unit.  Xi winding theory identifies local winding with multiplicity.
Those are genuine topological charges and need not vanish.  RH asks only that every such defect be
transversely uncharged.  Heights and multiplicities may have different local formation histories;
the universal law constrains the completion anomaly, not zero production.

This matches external anomaly language more closely than a particle-production metaphor:
solitonic defects may be charged under generalized symmetries, and the charge is the anomaly.
Here the fixed source reflection boundary is the candidate gauge relation, while its surviving
Roos class is the completion anomaly.

### Candidate universal principle: ancestral gauge exactness

`[BOLD CONJECTURE — CURRENT BEST BIG-PICTURE PRINCIPLE]`

> Arithmetic completion is a single source-defined, conservative derived functor, fixed before
> divisor selection.  It is continuous in the Lawson geometry of exact source relations.  Hence a
> literal gauge boundary cannot become a physical homology class at infinity, and a physical class
> cannot disappear by changing representatives.

Equivalent slogans:

- laws are ancestral to their solutions;
- physical completion may create analytic limits but not new gauge charge;
- exactness phase of a fixed source relation cannot jump under lawful completion;
- zeros are allowed topological defects, while off-line displacement is a forbidden completion
  anomaly.

This is stronger and more precise than “one common gauge.”  Common-gauge, dagger recovery,
Noether conservation, Tate self-duality, Hawking partner recovery, ABHN reciprocity, recurrence,
and spectral reality are shadows obtained after observing different coordinates of the same
exact-source profile.

### Whole-corpus synthesis under this principle

| Interfinity stable death geometry | Scott half: acquisition of a stable primitive |
| provenance exactness flags | lower half: survival of a fixed boundary class |

The last row explains why classic Hilbert, bounded-operator, finite-order, and scalar-reciprocity

minimal architectural requirement.

### External literature position

- Exact Tate categories provide extension-closed admissible Ind--Pro objects and index/central

  completion.
- Stable Tate and exact/hypercompletion theories retain derived information, but do not identify

No searched paper combines all four ingredients: fixed arithmetic source provenance, derived
Ind--Pro/bornological completion, two-sided Lawson exactness, and anomaly cancellation.  This
combination appears genuinely outside the classic RH route taxonomy.

### Hostile audit: why this is not yet an RH proof

1. `routeRPrimeGammaXiGlobalLawsonCompletionContinuous_iff_riemannHypothesis` proves the proposed
   continuity statement has exactly RH strength for the concrete Fisher source line.  Citing it as
   an axiom would be circular.
2. Finite exactness plus pointwise convergence is insufficient; this failure is now a theorem, not

3. Generic completion is not exact.  The explicit Roos class is the counterexample.
4. Generic Noether/Bianchi laws conserve or transport charge; they do not force zero charge.
5. Generic Tate self-duality and index theory retain defects; they do not annihilate them.
6. Covariant Stokes flatness holds at every grade after allowing the local-system differential to
   depend on that grade.  A fixed ancestral differential needs an independent arithmetic reason.
7. Reflection viewed only as a global symmetry permits off-line pairs or quartets.  To invoke gauge
   anomaly cancellation, one must prove that the literal source boundary is a genuine redundancy
   of the completed arithmetic theory, not relabel a global symmetry as gauge.
8. A topology defined to make exactness stable is not enough.  The actual prime--Gamma completion
   must be constructed in that topology and its continuity proved from source estimates or a
   universal exact-functor theorem.

### Exact open core

Build one pre-divisor completion functor, independent of Xi zeros, in a category capable of the
corpus's required nonlocal and singular behavior, and prove:

1. **chain-map law:** literal reflection/Gamma source differential commutes with completion;
2. **derived conservativity:** the functor is conservative on the rank-one Fisher boundary and its
   complementary primitive record;
3. **Lawson continuity:** every fixed source relation has eventually stable exactness phase;
4. **physical identification:** its completed target is the existing fixed-grade prime-tail Roos
   object, not a weaker orbit-paired or scalar quotient;
5. **non-circularity:** construction and continuity use no Xi zero, no zero location, no
   self-adjointness equivalent, and no assumed vanishing of the completion class.

Once those five are proved, current Lean chain closes RH immediately.  Work should now attack
existence of this nonlocal singular exact functor.  Another endpoint equivalence, positivity cone,
scalar conservation law, or state-dependent covariant differential will not advance the core.
