## 2026-08-23 — Hard reframing boundary: RH as a law of completion-born error

### Epistemic constraint

The age and resistance of RH are evidence against a short missing estimate inside an established
route.  They are not a proof that a simple proof is impossible, but they impose a research
discipline: no familiar representation of the problem may be treated as the ontology of the
problem.  In particular, zeros, a critical line, a spectrum, an operator, a positivity form, and a
named arithmetic or physical model are late readouts, not primitive objects.

It is impossible to certify the historical claim that nobody has considered any part of the
framework below.  The defensible novelty claim is narrower: the exact package of primitives,
admissible morphisms, falsifiers, and proof obligations below has not collided with the audited
corpus or literature so far.  Collision and priority remain open audits.

### Hard no-gos for the next framework

1. **NO ZERO-FIRST LANGUAGE.**  A zero set, divisor, zero-indexed basis, critical line, or Xi
   evaluation may identify a late physical observer.  None may define the carrier, topology,
   grading, charge, or admissible primitive.
2. **NO SPECTRAL PRODUCER.**  A self-adjoint operator, Hamiltonian, scattering pole, resonance, or
   Hilbert--Pólya realization cannot be the source of zeros.  Such an object can only be one scalar
   observer of an already existing completion defect.
3. **NO CLASSIC ROUTE AS UNIVERSAL CARRIER.**  Connes, Deninger, Suzuki, Hedenmalm, de Branges,
   Weil-positivity, trace-formula, and equivalent-criterion objects are projections of the sought
   law.  Rephrasing one of them in another route's notation is not progress on the source law.
4. **NO SINGLE POSITIVITY PRINCIPLE.**  Positivity is an observer-dependent Cartan reduction.
   Reflection-symmetric indefinite systems retain opposite charged defects, so positivity cannot
   be the primitive reason for neutrality.
5. **NO CONSERVATION-ALONE ARGUMENT.**  Noether, Bianchi, balance, determinant, and reflection-pair
   laws transport or conserve charge; they do not force charge to vanish.  The corpus contains
   conserved hyperbolic currents with nonzero paired charges.
6. **NO ABSOLUTE GAUGE FLATTENING.**  Countertransport that depends on the grade flattens every
   grade and erases the detector.  Only a relative raw-to-flat class with a fixed ancestral source
   can carry information.
7. **NO ENDPOINT OR POISSON COMPRESSION.**  The explicit theta shear lies in both endpoint kernels,
   survives Poisson transport, and has nonzero aperture trace.  Endpoint data are not faithful;
   even full Poisson exactness does not control a later change of completion.
8. **NO FINITE-EXACTNESS LEAP.**  Exactness at every finite source stage plus pointwise stabilization
   of every fixed marker does not imply exactness after singular completion.  Escaping primitives
   and nonuniform provenance are the phenomenon to be explained, not technical noise.
9. **NO ONE-NORM TOPOLOGY.**  A non-discrete norm permits cofinal private markers to escape; a norm
   uniformly faithful to all grades becomes discrete.  Required topology must remember a family
   of source tests and its exactness phases.
10. **NO PLAIN HILBERT COMPLETION.**  Hilbert completion admits the one-sided Green tail but can kill
    source functionals; the strong source topology retains the functionals but excludes that tail.
    The sought carrier must retain the relative relation between both, not select one side.
11. **NO GENERIC `lim^1` EXPLANATION.**  The finite-dimensional towers audited in the corpus are
    Mittag--Leffler and have trivial raw `lim^1`.  Any derived obstruction must be source-marked,
    topological, or bornological rather than the generic slogan “a derived-limit class appears.”
12. **NO TARGET-FITTED SOURCE.**  A carrier completed from Xi zeros, a zero divisor, or the desired
    evaluation bounds has assumed the phenomenon it claims to explain.
13. **NO EQUIVALENCE CATALOGUE AS PROOF.**  Many routes reporting the same scalar condition shows
    that they are lossy observers of common structure.  It does not identify or enforce the common
    source law.
14. **NO THEOREM-WRAPPER MECHANISM.**  A definition followed by “this property iff RH” is an
    endpoint, not an explanation, unless the property follows independently from source axioms
    that have non-RH examples and counterexamples.
15. **NO METAPHOR DEBT.**  “Defect,” “soliton,” “horizon,” “charge,” “ancestry,” and “birth” are
    admissible only when each receives a type, functoriality law, exact theorem, explicit falsifier,
    and Lean target.

These no-gos disqualify the current “fixed prime/Gamma physical action whose two Noether descents
share one gauge” wording.  It is still producer language, and gauge equality is only one shadow of
the missing law.

### Proposed new field: Ancestral Completion-Birth Geometry

**Working title, not a novelty claim.**  Ancestral Completion-Birth Geometry studies laws that are
exact at every bounded observation scale but acquire homology when the observer performs a
singular global completion.  It treats completion as an active change of what counts as a lawful
primitive, not as passive closure of a space.

Primitive data are:

1. a **law seed** `S`, built from literal finite source relations before any target divisor exists;
2. a directed **observer fabric** `O_a S`, where each observer forgets specified provenance while
   retaining a declared family of source markers;
3. the relative **law fibre** `K_a := hofib(S -> O_a S)`, which records the relation lost by that
   observer rather than only the observed scalar value;
4. a **reflection of ancestry**, exchanging the two orientations of source migration before any
   functional equation is scalarized;
5. a multiplicative transport character of a defect line, whose modulus defines **migration
   charge** without reference to a complex zero;
6. **ancestral exactness**: a completed boundary counts as exact only when it has a primitive whose
   provenance is inherited from the same cofinal source cone.  Existence of an unmarked Hilbert
   primitive is insufficient.

A **completion birth** is a relative class for which every bounded observer supplies compatible
local primitives, but every cofinal family of those primitives loses an ancestral marker, so the
class survives at the completion horizon.  It is an error created by incompatibility between
source law and global observation.  It is not an object emitted by a Hamiltonian or placed by a
local mechanism.

All zeta zeros are conjecturally late scalar reports of completion births.  A zero on the critical
line is therefore still an error/defect, not a correctly produced particle.  Its distinction is
that it is a **neutral defect**: it may retain multiplicity and local winding, hence a solitonic or
topological charge internal to the neutral stratum, while carrying no transverse migration charge.
An off-line zero would report a **charged completion birth**.  RH says every surviving global error
is neutral; it does not say there are no errors.

### Universal candidate law

**[CONJECTURE — ANCESTRAL NEUTRAL-BIRTH LAW]**  In a two-sided ancestrally complete law fabric,
reflection-compatible singular completion cannot create a persistent class whose transport
character has non-unit modulus.

The intended reason is neither positivity nor spectral reality.  For a non-unit character, one
transport orientation has a convergent one-sided Green contraction.  Reflection supplies the
opposite orientation.  Two-sided ancestral completeness must glue the contractions in the
relative law fibre while preserving their common source markers.  The charged class is then a
boundary in the ancestral sense.  At unit modulus neither one-sided contraction is forced to be
source-admissible, so neutral completion births can remain.

After, and only after, constructing the arithmetic observer, its scalar readout should identify
the transport modulus with the transverse coordinate `2 * Re(rho) - 1`.  The neutral stratum then
appears to that observer as `Re(rho) = 1/2`.  This is where the familiar critical line enters; it is
an output coordinate on the error field, not the geometry in which the law is formulated.

The classic and fringe routes become validation projections:

- spectral, scaling, and trace-formula constructions observe the transport character;
- boundary positivity and kernel criteria observe selected Cartan reductions of the law fibre;
- Noether/Bianchi formulas observe migration-charge conservation but omit ancestral admissibility;
- Hawking/KMS analogies observe the paired horizon contractions;
- ABHN/local--global constructions observe source compatibility but scalar reciprocity forgets
  provenance;
- quantum recurrence observes that every finite observer may recur while the lifted ancestral
  primitive escapes globally;
- source-marked Lawson topology observes markerwise phase stabilization, but not by itself the
  gluing of cofinal primitives.

None is allowed to define the law.  Their value is that mutually alien projections must all factor
through the same source-defined completion birth.

### Falsifier and circularity audit

This proposal fails if any of the following occurs:

1. a source-defined, two-sided ancestrally complete countermodel has a persistent non-unit charged
   birth;
2. the two reflected Green contractions exist only after forgetting source markers;
3. the arithmetic law seed or observer fabric cannot be built without Xi zeros or an equivalent
   boundedness assumption;
4. identifying birth classes with the analytic divisor requires an unproved completeness statement
   equivalent to RH;
5. “two-sided ancestral completeness” expands on audit to include charged-class vanishing by
   definition.

The fifth danger is greatest.  Therefore the axiom must first be defined on non-arithmetic examples
where it can be proved or refuted without any L-function, and the neutral-birth theorem must be a
genuine derived consequence.

### First dependency-closed theorem program

1. Define a route-independent `CompletionBirthDatum`: finite source markers, observer fibres,
   reflection, transport character, markerwise exactness profile, and ancestral admissibility.
2. Prove the **charged-birth contraction theorem** on paper: non-unit transport plus two-sided
   ancestral completeness constructs a marker-preserving relative primitive.  State hypotheses so
   charged countermodels violate a named hypothesis rather than the conclusion by fiat.
3. Give one finite/algebraic positive model and one topological escaping-marker countermodel.
4. Only after those proofs close, formalize the abstract structure and theorem in Lean.  Existing
   source-marked exactness profiles, Lawson completion, relative transgression, and Green-range-wall
   modules are components; their required joint structure is missing and must be built.
5. Construct an Euler--Gamma--theta law seed and observer fabric before the divisor, and prove its
   two-sided ancestral completeness from source identities and estimates.
6. Prove the late physical-identification theorem: completion births are exactly Xi evaluation
   defects and migration charge scalarizes to `2 * Re(rho) - 1`.
7. Apply the abstract neutral-birth law.  This final step is short only because the new field has
   carried the hard proof in Steps 2, 5, and 6.

### New exact open core

The former “construct a fixed action/gauge” core is retired as too close to classic producer
language.  New core is: construct a divisor-free arithmetic law seed whose observer completion is
two-sided ancestrally complete, and prove that this source property forces all completion-born
homology to have unit transport modulus.  No current corpus theorem establishes that implication or
the physical identification.  Those are the two non-circular proof obligations now worth attacking.

## 2026-08-23 — Hostile revision: errors as collisions of infinite causal histories

### Retraction of the first universal law

`ANCESTRAL NEUTRAL-BIRTH LAW` is retired in its stated form. “Two-sided ancestrally complete” is
not yet a source property strong enough to imply neutral charge.

The corpus gives decisive counterpressure:

1. literal two-sided theta completion can be an analytic unit at every Xi divisor while preserving
   the entire divisor charge;
2. source-preimage and target-attainment completion defects are independent;
3. reflected nonzero charge is compatible with an invariant indefinite cross form;
4. normal-cone blow-up can retain a collapsed source direction while amplifying its readout;
5. fixed mutually absolutely continuous source laws can realize opposite conditional observers on
   apertures whose mass tends to zero;
6. conditional information can diverge while its mass-weighted current tends to zero.

Thus reflection, two-sidedness, ancestry, positivity, conservation, normal-cone regularity, and
global contiguity do not jointly close the argument. Information can escape through admission
aperture.

### Additional hard no-gos

16. **NO TWO-SIDEDNESS AS A SELECTOR.** Reflection supplies a partner, not annihilation. A
    two-sided completion may retain full charged divisor.
17. **NO SOURCE/TARGET DEFECT CONFLATION.** Missing source primitives and unattained target values
    are independent homology coordinates and require separate control.
18. **NO NORMAL CONE AS A LAW.** Blow-up faithfully exposes rank transmutation but can increase
    source gain. It is a microscope, not a selection principle.
19. **NO CONTIGUITY WITHOUT ADMISSION.** Absolute continuity of fixed global laws does not prevent
    opposite conditional limits on events of vanishing mass.
20. **NO INFORMATION WITHOUT CURRENT.** Divergent conditional KL/Jeffreys work is irrelevant when
    aperture mass evacuates fast enough that mass times work tends to zero.
21. **NO UNWEIGHTED DEFECT BUDGET.** Total collision mass can be one while depth-weighted causal
    history diverges. Corpus has exact telescoping probability law `1/((n+1)(n+2))` as countermodel.
22. **NO ABSOLUTE-HISTORY FINITENESS.** Requiring every completed history to have finite action
    would delete critical defects together with off-line ones. Only reflected disagreement, not
    common infinite ancestry, may be required to have finite action.
23. **NO RAW PRODUCT OR HILBERT CLOSURE.** Product and Hilbert completions admit infinite-support
    histories that are limits of every finite observer while relaxed source action is infinite.
    Completion carrier must be relative and restricted.
24. **NO BOUNDED FORMATION DEPTH.** Uniformly bounded depth forbids every completion birth. Since
    zeta has zeros, bounded depth cannot be an arithmetic axiom.
25. **NO SCALAR PROVENANCE.** Every additive scalar provenance law factors through an
    observer-chosen potential. Primitive ancestry must remain group-, word-, or history-valued.
26. **NO COMPLETE LOCAL OR PERTURBATIVE CLASSIFIER.** Complete polynomial moments, all local
    Jacobi data, every perturbative bounded-observable jet, and all perturbative form-factor jets
    can agree while global recurrence differs. Missing invariant is a global collision hierarchy.
27. **NO DOUBLE-KMS ALIGNMENT AXIOM.** Purity, reflection, and two thermal marginals permit
    independent charge-conjugate sectors. Same-pair occupation alignment is extra structure, not
    a consequence of horizon physics.
28. **NO ALMOST-EVERYWHERE OBSERVER.** A single dark shell can be made bright without changing full
    `L2` scattering operator, while analytic completion detects a one-dimensional charged defect.
    Null-set quotienting erases exactly objects in question.
29. **NO FITTED RENEWAL APERTURE.** A Jacobi/Weyl renewal evaluated at a zero-dependent aperture
    reproduces chosen displacement but exerts no source-native pressure on it.
30. **NO FINITE-ORDER SHORTCUT.** Entire order one does not by itself bound causal-history action.
    Any growth-to-relative-action theorem must be proved from literal source and cannot be inferred
    from Hadamard factorization.

### Replacement field: Causal Collision Completion Geometry

**Working title, not a priority claim.** Primitive object is no longer a zero or isolated
completion class. It is a collision of two infinite source-formation histories that one completed
observer identifies.

For a source law, let:

1. `h+` and `h-` be completed causal histories, each obtainable as a limit of finite formation
   words but not required to have finite absolute history action;
2. `pi` be a source-independent observer and `pi(h+) = pi(h-)` an **observer collision**;
3. `tau` be a transverse ancestry quotient, defined before any Xi divisor, which forgets internal
   winding but retains migration direction;
4. `Delta := tau(h+) - tau(h-)` be relative transverse history;
5. `Abar(Delta)` be lower-semicontinuous relaxation of a proper weighted finite-history action;
6. source renewal be a cofinal translation of histories under which `Delta` is invariant.

A **lawful global error** is an observer collision with finite *relative transverse* action
`Abar(Delta)`. No finiteness is imposed on common mode of `h+` and `h-`. This is central change:
global completion may add an infinite causal end, but it may not manufacture an infinite
disagreement between its two reflected ancestries.

Carrier is therefore a relative restricted completion, schematically

`{(h+,h-) in Hbar x Hbar | tau(h+) - tau(h-) has finite relaxed source action}`,

not a Hilbert completion, product completion, spectrum, zero set, or quotient by almost-everywhere
equality. It resembles a restricted product only at set-shape level; restriction is generated by
causal formation weights and observer collision, not declared place by place.

### Paper-closed abstract law

**[THEOREM — RELATIVE RENEWAL RIGIDITY]** Let finite-support transverse histories be dense in a
product completion. Give them a proper positive weighted action whose bounded sublevels are finite,
and extend it by neighbourhood lower-semicontinuous relaxation. Then:

1. a completed relative history has finite relaxed action exactly when it comes from a
   finite-support transverse history;
2. if that relative history is invariant under a cofinal successor renewal, it is zero.

Proof. Existing weighted-history properness proves every product-completion point outside
finite-support range has infinite relaxation, while points in range retain finite action. Hence
finite relaxation puts `Delta` in finite-support range. Successor invariance makes all coordinates
of `Delta` equal. A constant finite-support history is zero. Therefore `tau(h+) = tau(h-)`.

Two hypotheses are independent:

- a one-coordinate spike has finite relative action but is not renewal-invariant;
- a nonzero constant mismatch is renewal-invariant but has infinite relaxed action;
- a common infinite history `(h,h)` has zero relative action even when `h` is outside
  finite-support range;
- histories may still differ in `ker(tau)`, allowing multiplicity, winding, and other internal
  topological defect data.

This theorem does not assume charge vanishing. It distinguishes common infinite ancestry from
transverse disagreement and proves neutrality from properness plus renewal.

### Reinterpretation of Riemann zeros

Every zero is conjecturally a different observer collision: two different infinite prime--Gamma
formation histories become same scalar completed response for a different causal reason. Zero is
aliasing error of completion, not an eigenvalue or excitation produced by a system.

A critical-line zero is a lawful neutral collision. Its two histories have identical transverse
renewal record but may differ by an internal kernel class. That residual class can carry
multiplicity and winding and is precise place where “topological soliton” may become literal. An
off-line zero would be an observer collision with a nonzero renewal-invariant transverse history.
Relative renewal rigidity says such a collision has infinite source action and cannot belong to
lawful relative completion.

Familiar coordinate `2 * Re(rho) - 1` must appear only in a late scalarization theorem as value of
transverse history. It cannot define `tau`, action, renewal, or completion carrier.

Classic and fringe programs now have sharply limited roles:

- Connes/Deninger/Hilbert--Polya report selected observer collisions or index shadows;
- Suzuki/Hedenmalm/Sonin/Weil report finite or scalar projections of collision action;
- Hawking/Hardy reports that an `L2`-invisible collision can retain winding at analytic horizon;
- quantum recurrence proves local and perturbative histories incomplete and identifies full
  collision hierarchy as genuinely global data;
- ABHN suggests restricted local-global admission, but scalar invariant sum is too coarse;
- source-marked Lawson topology records convergence of finite collision phases but not relative
  action properness;
- Jacobi/Weyl source renewal proves changing admission can preserve literal source capital, but a
  fitted aperture is only a shadow of intrinsic renewal law.

None is ontology. They are tests one divisor-free history construction must pass.

### Exact new open core

Abstract relative-renewal theorem is dependency-closed. RH now requires four source theorems, none
of which may mention a Xi zero in its construction:

1. **history construction:** build prime--Gamma finite-word formation system and its two completed
   reflected histories;
2. **collision landing:** prove every scalar Xi error is image of one observer collision, allowing
   different zeros to have different histories;
3. **relative properness and renewal:** prove collision has finite relaxed transverse action and
   transverse history is invariant under literal source renewal;
4. **late scalarization:** prove transverse history reads as `2 * Re(rho) - 1`, while internal
   kernel retains multiplicity/winding.

Steps 1 and 3 are new mathematical field. Step 2 is completeness, and Step 4 is identification.
Once all four are source-proved, relative renewal rigidity gives RH without a spectral producer,
positivity assumption, common gauge, or zero-first carrier.

### External collision audit

Terminology must be narrowed after literature comparison.

- Pairing two infinite configurations whose difference has finite support is already the
  **homoclinic/Gibbs relation** in symbolic and algebraic dynamics. It is not a new primitive.
- Barbieri--Labbé study **indistinguishable asymptotic pairs**: finite-pattern observers cannot
  distinguish two configurations beyond finitely supported rearrangement. Under uniform recurrence
  and a flip condition, their specific shift-space models lead to codimension-one cut-and-project
  descriptions. This is a close structural neighbour, not an RH theorem.
- Barbieri--García-Ramos--Li relate asymptotic pairs/homoclinic groups to entropy under Markovian and
  expansiveness hypotheses. Their carrier does not include source-weighted lower-semicontinuous
  relaxation or the arithmetic completion-birth problem.
- Gaiotto--Moore--Witten organize soliton sectors by asymptotic vacua, central charges, and web
  interactions. This supports history/interface-valued internal defect data but does not supply the
  relative-renewal selector.
- AQFT examples show relative entropy can support quantum soliton states even in topologically
  trivial sectors. Therefore “soliton” alone neither means nontrivial topology nor forces a charge
  classification.

Sources: `arXiv:2204.06413`, `arXiv:1911.00785`, `arXiv:1506.04087`, and
`arXiv:1907.10616`.

Defensible novelty target is therefore not finite-difference pairing. It is the exact combination
of (i) observer collision of completed arithmetic histories, (ii) unrestricted common infinite
ancestry, (iii) source-weighted relaxed transverse action, (iv) intrinsic cofinal renewal, and
(v) late Xi scalarization. No audited source combines these five ingredients.

2. `WeightedHistoryDatum.relativeHistoryRelaxation`;

## 2026-08-23 — Second revision: chain-recurrent errors and transverse dissipation

### Source-selector audit

The first collision formulation still made one dangerous move: it promoted pointwise successor
invariance of a transverse history to the universal source law. Corpus audit does not justify that.
A localized soliton may translate through history without being coordinatewise fixed. The theorem
is valid, but its renewal hypothesis is only one sufficient rigidity condition, not yet ontology.

Existing bilateral source mechanics are also classifiers, not construction of that hypothesis:

- `bilateral_geometricRefinementEnergy_summable_iff` says a positive geometric history and its
  reciprocal are both summable iff their radial ratio is one. It does not prove that a completed
  Xi error supplies both summable histories.
- `bilateralAllDegreeCompactAdmission_iff_zero` says one fixed source envelope rejects every
  nonzero reflected drift when all formation degrees are admitted. The same file constructs the
  adaptive-envelope countermodel; fixed decay is load-bearing and is not derived from completion.
- `bilateralInteractionAdmission_iff` isolates the mixed-exponent carrier, but its premise already
  quantifies summability of both shifted laws at every point above the wall.
- the literal second-Cartier source does construct both non-scalar orientations on every actual Xi
  fibre. It simultaneously proves that every strict-right image is nonmoderate. The missing order
  law is therefore real; simultaneous descent, Stokes flatness, and multiplicity fidelity do not
  imply it.
- the Gamma horizon constructs actual infinite histories, realizes every finite legal pole word,
  and is algebraically universal at dimension zero. It does not yet attach two such histories to
  one Xi error or supply a relative action ledger.
- exchangeable formation proves two-slot symmetrization blind and three-slot thinning detects the
  squared reflected mismatch. This supplies a candidate local action, but no global recurrence.

Therefore none of these theorems may be cited as the missing law. They are exact shadows that any
new law must recover.

### Further hard no-gos

31. **NO HOMOCLINIC QUOTIENT.** Quotienting histories by finite modification makes every quotient
    fibre dense in product topology and the induced quotient indiscrete. Any pullback-continuous
    reporter into a `T1` space is constant. Finite differences must remain charged arrows, not be
    identified as equal objects.
32. **NO BILATERAL CLASSIFIER AS ADMISSION.** An equivalence `both sides finite iff neutral` is not
    a proof that completion admits both sides. Using it backwards without a source theorem hides RH
    inside the word “admission.”
33. **NO STATIC SUCCESSOR FIXING AS UNIVERSAL LAW.** Coordinatewise shift invariance kills every
    finite-support transverse pattern. It is a useful sufficient theorem but excludes translating
    solitons before their internal and transverse coordinates are separated.
34. **NO DISSIPATION WITHOUT RECURRENCE.** A nonzero defect may decay forever under a contractive
    semigroup. Strict contraction alone does not forbid it.
35. **NO RECURRENCE WITHOUT DISSIPATION.** Periodic and almost-periodic systems carry arbitrary
    nonzero conserved charges. Recurrence alone does not select the critical line.
36. **NO PROJECTED RECURRENCE.** Return of a scalar, finite marginal, spectral form factor, or
    perturbative observer does not imply return of the lifted collision state or its action
    potential. Corpus contains exact recurrent completions invisible to all perturbative jets.
37. **NO IDEMPOTENCE SHORTCUT.** A completion projector may have an arbitrarily large fixed-point
    set. `C^2 = C` does not identify its physically silent kernel.
38. **NO TRIVIAL KERNEL.** Forcing every recurrent defect to be the zero object would erase
    multiplicity, winding, and soliton sector. Only transverse dissipative charge must vanish.
39. **NO RECURSIVELY DEFINED POTENTIAL.** Given any desired action one can manufacture a formal
    telescoping potential along a chosen orbit. Potential, transition, and action must be defined
    from prime--Gamma source before Xi landing.
40. **NO CONLEY RENAMING.** General Lyapunov/chain-recurrence theory says strict Lyapunov functions
    are constant on recurrent set. It does not identify arithmetic state space, causal transition,
    transverse action, or Xi landing.

Positive survivor of (31) is a **topological arrow groupoid with cost**: objects are completed
histories; arrows are finite-action relative histories; composition adds relative ancestry; common
infinite ancestry is gauge; finite arrows are never collapsed merely because they are homoclinic.
Positive survivor of (33)--(38) is a dynamical law that kills only dissipative transverse motion
and permits arbitrary motion inside its null kernel.

### Replacement universal law: recurrent completion dissipation

**Working law — CHAIN-RECURRENT COMPLETION ERROR LAW.** A lawful completion error is not a fixed
zero-like object. It is a chain-recurrent collision state in a source-defined causal completion
process. Each completion step has an exact ledger

`P(c_n) = P(c_(n+1)) + D_tau(c_n)`,

where `P` is global source potential and `D_tau >= 0` is action of transverse part of collision
arrow. Same unprojected source process returns cofinally in `P` to its starting value. Hence no
positive transverse action can occur at any step. Collision may nevertheless move inside
`ker(tau)`.

This is not ordinary Noether conservation. Noether begins with reversible continuous symmetry and
derives conserved charge. Here completion is an irreversible semigroup shadow. Proposed deeper
object is a reversible recurrent dilation whose observer compression has exact nonnegative action
loss. Conservation belongs to dilation; dissipation belongs to observer; their incompatibility on
a recurrent orbit forces transverse neutrality. Noether, KMS, scattering, positivity, and
recurrence become different projections of one dilation/compression law.

### Paper-closed dynamical theorem

**[THEOREM — RECURRENT COLLISION DISSIPATION RIGIDITY]** Let `c : Nat -> S` be collision states,
`P : S -> Real` a potential, and `D : S -> Real` a nonnegative transverse action. Assume

1. `P(c_n) = P(c_(n+1)) + D(c_n)` for every `n`;
2. for every `epsilon > 0` and every `K`, some `N >= K` satisfies
   `|P(c_N) - P(c_0)| < epsilon`.

Then `D(c_n) = 0` for every `n`.

Proof. Fix `k`. If `D(c_k) > 0`, choose a recurrent time `N >= k+1` whose potential return error
is below `D(c_k)/2`. Telescoping exact ledger gives

`P(c_0) - P(c_N) = sum_(j<N) D(c_j) >= D(c_k)`.

Absolute return error is strictly below `D(c_k)/2`, contradiction. Therefore every action term is
zero.

If `D_tau(h+,h-) = ||tau(h+ - h-)||^2`, theorem gives
`tau(h+) = tau(h-)` at every recurrent collision state, while `h+ - h-` may be any element of
`ker(tau)`. This is exact mathematical form of “every zero can fail for a different reason”: all
errors may carry different kernel/soliton classes while universal transverse radial error is zero.

Hypotheses are independent. Constant positive action with linearly decreasing potential has exact
accounting but no recurrence. Constant nonzero collision on periodic state has recurrence but no
dissipative ledger. A nontrivial kernel collision has zero transverse action and satisfies both.

### RH interpretation and exact source obligations

The already-formalized global Xi reflection-defect energy

`E_Xi = sum_q ||r(q) - conjugate(r(q))||^2`

is a late scalar shadow of total transverse collision action and satisfies `E_Xi = 0 iff RH`.
It is not new ontology and must not define source dynamics. New route succeeds only by proving,
before divisor landing, that source completion has a collision history whose recurrent action term
specializes to `E_Xi`.

Revised obligations:

1. **source causal process:** construct divisor-free prime--Gamma completed-history objects and a
   completion transition, preferably as observer compression of a reversible dilation;
2. **relative arrow carrier:** prove collision arrows have source-weighted finite relative action
   without quotienting finite differences or requiring finite absolute histories;
3. **exact action ledger:** derive one fixed source potential and exact step decomposition; do not
   define potential from desired defect;
4. **unprojected recurrence:** prove cofinal return of same lifted source potential/state, not of a
   scalar shadow or fitted aperture;
5. **collision landing:** only afterward identify actual Xi errors with collision arrows and prove
   total transverse action is `E_Xi` (or endpoint equivalent);
6. **kernel classification:** show residual null arrows retain multiplicity/winding and may vary
   from zero to zero.

Obligations (3) and (4) are universal law. Obligation (5) is arithmetic interpretation. Their
separation blocks the usual trick of encoding RH in an “admission,” “moderation,” or “recurrence”
definition.

### Literature boundary

Conley theory already places recurrent dynamics in zero set of strict Lyapunov descent; quantum
Markov theory already separates contractive directions from a decoherence-free algebra; homoclinic
dynamics already treats finite-difference configuration pairs. None supplies arithmetic causal
completion, unrestricted common ancestry with charged finite arrows, source-derived action ledger,
recurrent dilation, and late Xi collision energy in one construction. Novelty target is this full
six-part architecture, not abstract recurrence-plus-Lyapunov lemma.

Relevant external checks: `arXiv:0710.5683`, `arXiv:2011.09830`, `arXiv:2102.04146`,
`arXiv:1712.00637`, and `arXiv:2210.17513`.

`HTGeometry/CausalCollisionRecurrenceLaw.lean` formalizes transverse collision charge/action,

## 2026-08-23 — Source intersection: recurrence amplification of relative errors

Corpus contains a concrete divisor-free intersection, but not yet full RH bridge.

1. `GammaSewnMobiusJuliaSourceTransport.lean` constructs the unique minimal positive two-channel
   Julia lift of the scalar Gamma-sewn Möbius contraction. It is an exact plane isometry. At every
   positive aperture its environment coordinate is nonzero, and its boundary tangent is literal
   prime logarithm.
2. `ConstraintMotiveRelativity/PrimePhasePortRecurrence.lean` proves arbitrary late approximate
   return of every finite prime phase packet.

This kills one naive use of recurrent dissipation. Absolute Julia-environment energy cannot be
the error action: it is positive for every prime at every positive aperture, including neutral
sectors. It is common source radiation. A recurrent exact ledger charging this absolute energy
would contradict the source isometry itself. Therefore source action must be relative between two
colliding histories so their common Julia radiation cancels.

### Hard no-gos added

41. **NO ABSOLUTE JULIA DEFECT ACTION.** Minimal unitary dilation of a strict source contraction
    necessarily has nonzero environment energy. Charging it as error would reject every completed
    state, not only off-line errors.
42. **NO FINITE-APERTURE RECURRENCE AS GLOBAL RETURN.** Prime phase recurrence synchronizes any
    fixed finite packet. It does not control infinite prime tail or renormalized Gamma clock.
43. **NO REVERSIBLE ORBIT WITH ONE-WAY COST.** Full isometric dilation permits environment energy
    to return to visible channel. Stepwise nonnegative dissipation arises only after irreversible
    observation/reset and cannot be assigned to raw reversible orbit.
44. **NO COMMON-RADIATION BUDGET.** Summability or finiteness must apply to difference of two
    collision histories. Absolute Hawking/Julia/Gamma radiation may be infinite and recurrent.

Positive survivor is **recurrence amplification**. One off-line collision is not isolated: source
phase dynamics replays its finite patterns arbitrarily late. If completed relative history has
finite total action, infinitely late approximate replicas of one strictly positive transverse
error cannot all be paid.

### Paper-closed recurrence-amplification theorem

**[THEOREM — COFINAL COLLISION REPLICATION RIGIDITY]** Let `q_n` be transverse charges of a
sequence of relative collision arrows in a normed group and assume

1. `sum_n ||q_n||^2 < infinity`;
2. a charge `q` is replayed cofinally: for every `epsilon > 0` and every `N`, some `n >= N`
   satisfies `||q_n - q|| < epsilon`.

Then `q = 0`.

Proof. Summability forces `||q_n|| -> 0`. If `||q|| > 0`, choose
`epsilon = ||q||/3`. Beyond one horizon every `||q_n|| < epsilon`, while cofinal replay gives an
`n` there with `||q_n-q|| < epsilon`. Triangle inequality yields
`||q|| < 2||q||/3`, contradiction.

Unlike coordinatewise successor invariance, theorem permits a localized soliton to move through
history. Unlike potential-recurrence theorem, it needs no monotone state potential. Load-bearing
inputs are finite *total relative* action and recurrence of same unprojected transverse charge.
Kernel-valued internal class again survives.

This suggests revised physical picture: prime--Gamma completion is a recurrent radiation bath;
zeros are collision events inside it. Critical zeros are cost-free topological replay classes.
An off-line zero would be a positive-cost error replayed cofinally by source phases, generating
infinite relative action and therefore excluded from completed collision carrier.

Remaining source work is now narrower but severe: extend finite-prime phase recurrence to the
relative prime--Gamma collision topology with a summable-tail theorem, prove different replay
events occupy distinct action coordinates, and identify their charge only afterward with
`2 * Re(rho) - 1`. Finite-aperture Kronecker recurrence alone does not cross this gap.

Lean theorem
`collisionTransverseCharge_eq_zero_of_summableAction_of_cofinallyRecurrent` formalizes the full
normed-group statement. Targeted build passes; axiom audit reports only `propext`,
`Classical.choice`, and `Quot.sound`.

## 2026-08-23 — Third revision: clock-locked recurrent dilation

Countable source recurrence is no longer the gap. `SummablePurePointPhaseRecurrence.lean` now

Hostile audit shows why this still does not produce RH. Source phase recurrence returns the

The closest existing Xi shadow,
`XiHorizontalCollisionPrimeCayleyInformationLoss.lean`, recovers horizontal collision charge as a

`0,2,4/3`. Information flows back, so the sequence is not monotone. A Cesaro limit cannot be

### Hard no-gos added

45. **NO CESARO LOSS AS ENTROPY PRODUCTION.** A positive asymptotic dephasing defect may have
    finite-depth backflow. The word “information loss” does not supply data processing.
46. **NO SPATIAL BUDGET REUSED IN TIME.** A summable prime or prime-depth trace budget cannot pay
    infinitely many recurrent events unless distinct returns are proved orthogonal, disjoint, or

47. **NO BACKGROUND RETURN AS ERROR REPLAY.** Return of the fixed prime--Gamma phase packet does not
    imply return of a marked transverse collision charge. The marking and its continuity must be

48. **NO DIVISOR-PARAMETERIZED DYNAMICS AS SOURCE INDEPENDENCE.** Evaluating a source embedding at
    an Xi zero is legitimate late landing. Using the zero to define the transition, weight,
    potential, channel, or recurrence clock is not an upstream law.
49. **NO TWO-CLOCK GRAFT.** Unitary source time and observer depth cannot be chosen independently.
    Recurrence on one clock plus monotonicity on another has no rigidity consequence.
50. **NO AUTOMATIC MARKOV SHADOW OF A RECURRENT DILATION.** Reduced dynamics of a recurrent closed
    system may have memory and information backflow. Time-homogeneous data processing must be

51. **NO TRACE-CLASS RECURRENCE AS COLLISION LANDING.** The cubic source carrier closes countable
    recurrence and finite total background mass, but neither statement identifies its loss with
    the Xi reflection defect. That identification remains a theorem, not terminology.

Positive survivor is a stricter synthesis:

**Working law — CLOCK-LOCKED RECURRENT DILATION LAW.** A lawful completion has one physical clock
and two compatible descriptions:

1. a reversible prime--Gamma dilation whose complete source state returns cofinally;
2. a time-homogeneous observer channel on relative collision states, intertwined with that same
   clock, whose operational distinguishability obeys data processing.

Because the source states recur and the observer is a fixed image of them, the same operational
distance returns cofinally to its initial value. Because the channel makes that distance
antitone, it must be constant. Every observer-loss increment is therefore zero. The lawful
completion sector is the decoherence-free/reversible part of the observer channel; off-line
transverse errors would be irreversible memory-loss events and are excluded. Kernel-valued
topological solitons may still differ from zero to zero because zero loss means reversible
transport, not equality of internal histories.

This is not Connes's trace formula, Deninger's flow, Hilbert--Polya spectrum, Suzuki positivity, or
Hedenmalm cyclicity. Those become scalar, spectral, cohomological, or kernel shadows of one
nomological object: a recurrent source law together with its lawful lossy observers. Zeros are not
states created by that object. They are attempted observer-memory defects. Critical zeros are
defect-free internal solitons; a hypothetical off-line zero is a permanent loss that the same
source clock must later reconstruct, which a Markov observer cannot do.

### Paper-closed operational theorem

Let `d_n` be operational distinguishability of two states after `n` iterations of one channel.
Data processing makes `d_n` antitone. If a strictly increasing sequence `phi(j)` satisfies
`d_(phi(j)) -> d_0`, then `d_n=d_0` for every `n`, hence information loss `d_0-d_n` is zero at every
depth. Proof: for fixed `n`, antitonicity gives `d_n <= d_0`; eventually `phi(j)>=n`, so
`d_(phi(j))<=d_n`; taking the recurrent limit gives `d_0<=d_n`.

`UniversalOperationalMemoryFloor.lean` now formalizes this as
`distanceAt_eq_initial_of_subseq_return` and
`informationLossAt_eq_zero_of_subseq_return`. The result is axiom-clean modulo `propext`,
`Classical.choice`, and `Quot.sound`.

### Exact remaining construction

One commutative square is now the open core. Construct, without Xi divisor data,

`source dilation at time n -> source dilation at time n+1`

whose fixed observer image is

`relative collision state at depth n -> relative collision state at depth n+1`,

then prove:

1. fixed cubic prime--Gamma recurrence lifts from scalar phase energy to recurrence of the full
   marked relative state;
2. observer transition is time-homogeneous and contractive on a separating operational effect
   family;
3. its information loss is finite relative action, not absolute Julia/Hawking radiation;
4. late Xi landing identifies total transverse loss with `xiGlobalReflectionDefectEnergy` (or the
   exact horizontal collision charge);
5. zero-loss sector retains multiplicity and topological kernel class.

Items 1--3 are new universal mathematics. Item 4 is arithmetic interpretation. Existing corpus
proves the abstract rigidity, fixed countable prime--Gamma recurrence, exact Xi collision shadows,
and late RH endpoints separately; it does not yet prove this square.

## 2026-08-23 — Fourth revision: completion-scale 2-geometry

### 150-year filter

Any proposed solution whose primitive object is already a spectrum, trace formula, positivity
kernel, cohomology group, scattering determinant, zero-counting current, or scalar recurrence is
rejected as ontology. Those languages have had enough expressive power to restate RH for decades.
They may be receivers of a proof, but they cannot be the new law. A viable proposal must explain
why all those inequivalent receivers exist as decategorified shadows, why they share the same
missing orientation, and why the arithmetic completion permits no transverse defect while
retaining arbitrary local zero multiplicity.

The previous one-clock proposal fails this filter and also fails formally. The literal
von-Mangoldt/Gamma radial source shift in `VonMangoldtGammaHilbertClockDescent.lean` has operator
norm at most `1/2`. `StrictContractionRecurrenceNoGo.lean` now proves, without linearity or finite
dimension, that a strictly geometrically contracting orbit which returns cofinally to its initial
state must be zero. Its source specialization
`vonMangoldtGammaSource_eq_zero_of_sourceShift_cofinal_return` proves that no nonzero fixed-carrier
von-Mangoldt/Gamma source vector can use radial shift as its recurrent clock. The prime-log vertical
phase clock and radial completion shift are not two descriptions of one real evolution.

### Hard no-gos added

52. **NO ONE-REAL-CLOCK COMPLETION.** Radial source translation is a strict contraction; vertical
    prime-log translation is recurrent and unitary. Identifying their integer parameters forces
    every recurrent source vector to vanish.
53. **NO FIXED POSITIVE HILBERT CARRIER FOR BOTH RADIAL DIRECTIONS.** Corpus bilateral-dilation
    rigidity proves nonunit expansion and its inverse cannot both act boundedly on one carrier.
    Reflection across radial grade must change positive topology or pass through an indefinite
    comparison object.
54. **NO ORDINARY COMPLEX-TIME FLATNESS.** Horizontal and vertical scalar translations commute
    tautologically. `PairGroupoidBidescentLaw.lean` proves exact vertical gluing can preserve an
    arbitrary horizontal defect. Cauchy--Riemann compatibility alone has no RH content.
55. **NO UNITARY-DILATION ESCAPE.** A unitary dilation stores strict-contraction loss in an
    environment. Recurrence of full dilated state neither makes reduced dynamics Markovian nor
    makes observer distinguishability return to its pre-observation value. It relocates the seam.
56. **NO REVERSIBLE/STABLE DECOMPOSITION AS SELECTOR.** Semigroup peripheral/transient
    decompositions classify where recurrence can live, but do not identify the arithmetic
    completion seam or force a given divisor current into reversible sector.
57. **NO ZERO-AS-VORTEX CLAIM BY ITSELF.** Poincare--Lelong turns every holomorphic zero into a
    positive divisor current. It records multiplicity but gives no reason that support lies on
    reflection fixed locus. Topological charge is payload, not confinement law.
58. **NO NOETHER LAW WITHOUT A TRANSVERSE SEAM.** Continuous translation symmetry can conserve a
    source current. Reflection is discrete, and ordinary conserved charge can transport an
    arbitrary reflection-odd defect. Required object is a mixed seam anomaly with a positive
    transverse radical, not another conserved scalar.
59. **NO FLAT ENDPOINT STORY.** Trivial triangle holonomy reconstructs an endpoint-indexed gauge,
    but an arbitrary horizontal error may be built into that gauge. Flatness of raw transport is
    not vanishing of completed reflection 2-holonomy.
60. **NO DIAGONAL CHARACTER FLATNESS.** Literal opposite prime characters have flat diagonal
    product while their paired nonlinear curvature is strictly positive off neutral grade.
    First-order cancellation misses exact second-order obstruction.
61. **NO UNMARKED DEFECT CONFINEMENT.** Summable phase recurrence replays background. A confinement
    theorem needs source-defined marked 2-cells retaining prime/depth, seam orientation, local
    winding, and multiplicity. Otherwise different zeros are silently identified.

Positive survivor of 52--61 is not another route. It is a candidate new mathematical category.

### Big-picture object — COMPLETION-SCALE 2-GEOMETRY

A completion source should be a smooth or analytic *field* of Hilbert spaces `H_x`, indexed by
radial completion grade `x`, not one Hilbert space. It carries four kinds of data:

1. **vertical recurrence morphisms** `U_x(t) : H_x -> H_x`, unitary on the prime-log direction;
2. **radial completion morphisms** `C_(x,y) : H_x -> H_y`, contractive for `x < y`, with composition
   but no bounded inverse on one fixed positive carrier;
3. **completion/reflection seams** comparing forward and reflected radial histories only after
   Euler and Gamma completion;
4. **marked 2-cells** comparing the two composite paths around a radial--vertical--reflection
   rectangle. Their failure to agree is completion 2-holonomy.

Ordinary source translation may be flat. RH-bearing object is not its connection curvature. It is
the *relative 2-holonomy of the completion seam*: failure of reflection descent to commute with
radial completion and vertical recurrence after changing Hilbert topology. Existing
`eventSeamDefect`, `eventTriangleDefect`, and their Bianchi identity are algebraic seeds; they do
not yet construct this analytic Hilbert field or its arithmetic seam.

For transverse grade `a`, corpus already exposes canonical positive seam tension

`kappa_p(a) = 2 * (cosh(a * log p) - 1)`,

whose radical is exactly `a = 0`, while diagonal opposite-character transport remains flat. This
is the correct shape: flat underlying source motion, positive completed 2-cell tension. It is not
yet the global action law because current occurrences are evaluated at divisor-selected grades.

### Working law — RECURRENT COMPLETION-DEFECT CONFINEMENT

Construct marked completion 2-cells before any Xi divisor is selected and prove:

1. every cell has nonnegative *relative* seam action; common Gamma/Julia/Hawking radiation cancels;
2. total action of a completed relative history is finite;
3. vertical source recurrence replays each marked 2-holonomy cofinally in topology retaining its
   internal mark;
4. transverse action vanishes exactly on neutral radial grade, while zero action leaves a kernel
   containing winding and multiplicity;
5. only after 1--4, Xi zeros land as topological cells, radial grade lands as
   `Re(rho)-1/2`, and kernel class lands as local multiplicity/winding.

Then every replayed positive-tension off-wall cell would consume positive action arbitrarily late,
contradicting finite total relative action. Every Xi defect is therefore confined to neutral wall.
Critical zeros are not generated objects. They are zero-tension topological completion defects:
each may retain a different internal reason, winding, multiplicity, and local environment history.
RH asserts only universal *transverse confinement*, not common microscopic production.

This reframes all current routes as shadows. Spectral realizations see linearized monodromy of
zero-tension sector; positivity criteria see nonnegativity of seam tension; trace formulas see its
decategorified total; cohomological pictures see kernel classes; Hawking/Julia constructions see
common environment radiation which must cancel from relative action; ABHN-style reciprocity sees
the global cancellation constraint on local marked cells; quantum recurrence supplies vertical
replay. None alone contains completion-scale 2-holonomy.

### Exact non-circular open core

1. Define radial Hilbert field directly from coefficient-faithful von-Mangoldt/Gamma density and
   prove smooth/analytic field structure without a global fixed-carrier trivialization.
2. Lift prime-log vertical phase action to unitary maps on every radial fiber and prove covariance
   with radial contractions.
3. Construct reflection/completion seam from Euler--Gamma source data, not Xi zeros, and form its
   marked 2-holonomy.
4. Prove an exact nonnegative relative-action/Bianchi identity whose radical is neutral grade.
5. Prove cofinal recurrence of the *marked seam cell*, not background phase energy.
6. Prove finite total temporal action without reusing one spatial trace budget at every return.
7. Land completed Xi divisor current and local multiplicity only after source confinement theorem.

Items 1--6 define new field and its confinement theorem. Item 7 is arithmetic interpretation. Any
candidate importing zero locations, divisor copies, or an RH-equivalent positivity statement into
1--6 is circular and rejected.

### External boundary audit

Existing literature supplies components, not synthesis. Holomorphic contraction semigroups admit
unitary dilations in larger (including Krein) spaces (`arXiv:1811.02088`), but dilation does not
identify completion seam action. Smooth fields of varying `L2` spaces and their connections are
available (`arXiv:2308.04066`), but no arithmetic completion field or recurrence-confinement law is
provided. Generalized Poincare--Lelong theory represents zeros by supported currents
(`arXiv:math/0412446`), but has no reflection-wall confinement. Jacobs--de Leeuw--Glicksberg theory
splits reversible and almost weakly stable semigroup sectors (`arXiv:2509.24003`), but does not
source which arithmetic defect belongs to which sector. Novelty target is the combined marked
completion-scale 2-geometry and its finite-action recurrent confinement theorem; full literature
novelty audit remains open.

`HTGeometry/StrictContractionRecurrenceNoGo.lean` formalizes geometric-decay recurrence collapse

but must reject a free reflection pair.

All-rank Euler--Wick divisor theorem corrects naive proposal. Genuine meromorphic roots of
unbounded degree annihilate local divisor order at *any* point. A root tower imposed on whole
completed divisor would erase Hardy's known critical-line zeros. Divisibility can act only on

### Big-picture object — REFLECTION-EQUIVARIANT ARITHMETIC COMPLETION SEMANTICS

Proposed primitive object is neither function, spectrum, operator, action, flow, trace, cohomology
group, nor positivity cone. It is a source-defined infinitary rule system whose objects are finite
prime--Gamma completion states and whose legal rewrites are local arithmetic completion operations.
Relations between rewrites are retained as higher cells rather than quotiented away. Reflection
acts on entire rule system.

Finite stages need not be confluent. Each unresolved critical branching is an *error record*.
Repairing one branching can expose next, so different zeros may encode genuinely different critical
histories. Completed object is a reflection-equivariant minimal coherent resolution: contractible
free branch pairs are removed, while irreducible fixed-isotropy higher cells survive. Those
surviving cells are proposed topological solitons. Internal higher-cell class, winding, and
multiplicity may differ arbitrarily; only transverse reflection displacement is forced to vanish.

In this language RH does not say one mechanism produces every zero. It says prime--Gamma completion
grammar has no minimal critical generator in a free reflection orbit. Critical-line zeros are
legitimate irreducible syzygies of completion law. A hypothetical off-line pair is a contractible
critical branching incorrectly retained as a generator: a global completion error.

Known routes become semantics/reporters. Positivity measures nonnegative critical-pair valuation;
trace formulas decategorify inventory; spectra linearize rewrite monodromy; Tate and geometric
fixed-point constructions read isotropy; recurrence inserts a branch into late contexts;
Hawking/Julia channels record discarded environment cells; ABHN reads local/global exactness;
Euler--Wick gauges supply independent formation refinements; Noether laws report rewrite
invariants. None is rule system itself.

### Paper-closed algebraic survivor

Let `I` carry involution `sigma`, and let `q : I ->_0 Z` be finite integral defect charge. Write
`q_fix` for restriction to fixed labels followed by extension by zero and
`q_free = q - q_fix`. Assume only `q_free`, not `q`, admits divisors of unbounded size in the
*original* charge lattice:

`for every N, there exist n >= N and h : I ->_0 Z with n h = q_free`.

Then `q_free = 0`, hence `q` is supported on fixed locus. Proof is coefficientwise. At each label
`i`, hypothesis makes integer `q_free(i)` divisible by integers of unbounded size; therefore it is
zero. No fixed coefficient is divided, so arbitrary fixed-locus multiplicity survives. For a
nonzero point charge `m [rho]`, conclusion forces `sigma(rho)=rho`.

This theorem is not RH and assumes no analytic source. It is exact missing algebraic interface
between corpus fixed/free localization and unbounded formation-root selection. New arithmetic task:
construct compatible unbounded prime--Gamma formation divisors for *transverse free part* of
completed source defect before Xi divisor selection.

### Working conjecture — SOURCE TRANSVERSE-DIVISIBILITY LAW

**[CONJECTURE]** Literal Euler prime-power insertion, Gamma renormalization, functional-equation
reflection, and independent prime-gauge formation assemble into a reflection-equivariant analytic
polygraph with integral completed-defect valuation. In its minimal coherent completion, every
free/reflection-transverse defect chain admits compatible formation division of unbounded degree in
original integral chain lattice, whereas fixed-isotropy higher cells are retained unchanged. Late
divisor landing sends transverse chain to free part of Xi divisor.

If constructed, paper-closed survivor annihilates free Xi divisor and proves RH while preserving
every critical-line zero and multiplicity. Main circularity risk is severe: claimed source division
may secretly choose Xi roots or assume fixed-locus landing. Each formation divisor must be built
coefficient-faithfully from prime--Gamma rules and checked before any zero is named.
